# NSH parity checklist (GPUI desktop → NSH)

```yaml
assignment: a20829f1-3cd6-48d3-b100-5c4fc07a7823
branch: nsh-contracts
source_revision: 50b3b412d77b95a0637c7d72bb7e6d475b50e349
verification_environment: code_and_documentation_reading_only
tests_executed: none
live_targets_touched: none
task_status: not_marked_done
```

This is the list of everything the GPUI desktop (`crates/riwork-desktop`, binary `riwork`) does
that the NSH port must keep, derived from its code and tests, the `GPUI_KIT_*` contracts and
receipts, `README.md` and `docs/`. Each row is traced to the code that implements it today and
to the tests that pin it, and is mapped to the NSH surface that will implement it. The seams
those surfaces meet at are frozen in [`docs/nsh-contracts.md`](nsh-contracts.md); terminal and
layout behaviors are specified in more depth by the sibling spec
`docs/nsh-spec-terminal-layout.md` (branch `nsh-spec-terminal-layout`, commit `a3bc62d`,
row IDs `T1`…`G4`).

The checklist describes the code at `source_revision`. Row text says what the code does, not
what a doc wishes it did; where docs and code disagree it is noted in the row and listed under
[Doc/code disagreements](#doccode-disagreements-to-settle-before-porting).

## How to read a row

### Phone and desktop pass — 2026-10-28

The `nsh` worktree received a source-based feature/design pass across Projects,
Files/Preview, Global/Dashboard, status controls, Chat, Settings, Schedules and
Remote, plus the iOS NSH shell and its retained native screens. This does not
mark the historical checklist's verification rows complete. No test, lint or
type-check suites were run for this pass.

Concrete restorations include persisted project ordering, Files keyboard focus,
status-menu keyboard access, direct dashboard decision review, desktop theme
choices, New Chat provider/location selection, explicit `/memo` insertion and
chat snippet actions. Composer suggestions reuse the existing engine catalogs.
Suggestion selection uses clicks and native Return handling; unmodified
arrow/Tab/Escape interception is omitted because NSH 0.3 cannot synchronously
exempt IME marked text in its global key monitor. File suggestions show metadata
without fetching file-content previews.

iPhone always uses Native appearance and follows its own light/dark setting;
iPad retains theme sync. Mobile dashboard filters, grouped project navigation,
saved-read indicators, decision coverage, keyboard routes and failure recovery
were restored in the NSH shell. Chat, terminal, pairing, settings and preview
interactions continue through their existing native implementations. See
[the iOS migration boundary](nsh-ios-migration.md) for what still lives in Swift.

### Original checklist columns

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ID | `SHELL-*` shell and windows, `TERM-*` terminals, files, remote, previews, `CHATT-*` chat transcript, `CHATC-*` chat composer and chat chrome, `PANEL-*` panels, settings, dashboard, controls, `DOCS-*` requirements stated in contracts, receipts, README and docs. IDs are stable; a row that turns out wrong is struck through, never renumbered. |
| Behavior to preserve | One checkable statement with the exact values the code uses. |
| GPUI source | Repo-relative `path:line` of the implementation at `source_revision`. |
| Tests | `path:line test_name` of tests that assert it today, or `none`. Tests were read, not run. |
| NSH surface | Where the behavior lives after the port (vocabulary below). |
| Verify | How the port proves it: `core` (logic stays in riwork-core/engine; the existing tests keep covering it), `bridge` (Rust test at the `riwork/native` boundary), `nsh-test` (headless TSX test with `nsh test`), `host` (AppKit host / native component integration test, isolated `RIWORK_HOME`), `manual` (checked by a person; add automation where possible). |
| Notes | Risks, edge cases, budgets, doc references. |

A row is **done** when its Verify route passes against the NSH build. Rows tagged `GAP:` cannot
be done on NSH 0.3 as it is; they are collected in the [gap register](#gap-register).

### NSH surface vocabulary

| Tag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `TSX:<Name>` | App-owned TSX built from NSH primitives (`View`, `Text`, `Scroll`, `Input`, `List`, `Image`, `Canvas`, `Overlay`, `WebView`, `Video`) and `nsh/base` (`Button`, `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `Radio`, `Menu`/`ContextMenu`/`Submenu`, `Popover`, `Tooltip`, `Tabs`, `Sheet`, `Dialog`/`AlertDialog`, `SelectionArea`, `NavStack`). |
| `nsh/base:<Component>` | nsh-base 0.3 ports of GPUI Kit: `DockArea`, `ResizablePanelGroup`, `Editor`, `DiffView`, `TextView` (Markdown/HTML/blocks, `streaming`), `DataTable`, `Table`, `Toast`, `Plot`, `Collapsible`. |
| `nsh/desktop`, `nsh/keymap`, `nsh/clipboard`, `onFileDrop`, `nsh/device`, `nsh/accessibility`, `nsh/animated` | NSH built-in modules and props of each AppView. |
| `riwork/native:<Module>.<fn>` | The Rust UI set of [contracts §1](nsh-contracts.md#1-riworknative-module-surface). |
| `riwork.terminal …` | The native terminal component of [contracts §3](nsh-contracts.md#3-riworkterminal-native-component) (`prop:`, `event:`, `cmd:`). |
| `host:<name>` / `host:command:<id>` | The AppKit host channel of [contracts §2](nsh-contracts.md#2-appkit-host-channel-per-window): `host.params`, `host.call`, `host.on`, and app/Dock-menu commands. Readers' working names are mapped to the frozen names in [Host needs → frozen names](#host-needs--frozen-host-names). |
| `GAP:<what>` | Nothing in NSH 0.3 covers it: needs upstream NSH work, host code, or a native component. |
| `n/a-NSH` | A GPUI-specific workaround that disappears; the row says what must still hold. |

## Method and verification record

- Six readers inventoried disjoint file sets of `crates/riwork-desktop/src` (shell and windows;
  terminals, files, remote and previews; chat transcript; chat composer; panels, settings and
  dashboard) and the written contracts/docs. The design owner wrote the contracts from the NSH
  0.3 crate sources, `riwork-core`/`riwork-engine`, the sibling spec and the two spike reports,
  and cross-checked every reader's “Bridge needs” and “Host needs” against them.
- Every `path:line` citation in this file was machine-checked against the files at
  `source_revision` (file exists, line in range), and every `path:line test_name` pair was
  checked to name a `fn test_name` within three lines of the cited line. Counts are in
  [Verification record](#verification-record). Behavior statements were read from code;
  nothing was built, run, or tried in a live window.
- [Coverage map](#coverage-map) lists every source file of `crates/riwork-desktop/src` and the
  sections that cite it.

## Port gates

Rows that block switching the default desktop to NSH regardless of how many other rows pass.

| Gate | Rows | Status at Step 0 |
|---|---|---|
| Terminal teardown never blocks the main thread; detach before free; active-tmux disposal test | contracts §3.7, TERM lifecycle rows, spec T1/T7 | **Open.** Ghostty spike NO-GO (`018a535`): `ghostty_surface_free` blocked the main thread > 47 s until the tmux client was detached. |
| A tab moved between panes keeps its `riwork.terminal` instance (no drop, no re-attach) | spec T6, TERM attach rows, contracts §3.5 | Open; spike proved retained-view reattach, not DockArea re-parenting. |
| Terminals stay below NSH overlays (or `frozen` snapshots work) | SHELL-112, spec T6, contracts §3.2 `frozen` | Open; needs a host check. |
| Matched streaming benchmark vs GPUI (same workload, profile, viewport, frame boundary) | [Performance baselines](#perf-baselines-to-beat-p-docs), CHATT perf rows | Open. Host spike GO (`6475d17`): 475-chunk answer in two windows, surface p95 3.0-4.7 ms, no retained frame > 16.67 ms; no matched comparison. |
| Composer input hooks: key interception before the field, IME-composition state, cancellable paste, key repeat/up | CHATC “Composer text editing & keys”, DOCS inputs | Open; `GAP` in NSH `Input`/`Editor` (see gap register). |
| Layout: locked panes keep pixel size, empty main pane kept, RiWork drop zones | SHELL panes/drag rows, contracts §4 | Contract rules cover locked panes and empty panes; root-edge/top drop zones are an upstream `GAP`. |
| Single instance per `RIWORK_HOME` and reload hand-over rebuilt in the host | SHELL startup/restart rows, contracts §0/§2.5 | Design decided (contracts §0); not built. |
| No per-window or per-frame polling; every poll once per process with change events | SHELL/PANEL/TERM polling rows, DOCS watch/polling rows, contracts §1.3 | Design decided; inventory in contracts §1.3. |

## Shell & window (P-SHELL)

Scope: `crates/riwork-desktop/src/` main.rs (app shell), pane_layout.rs, tab_strip.rs, tab_menu.rs, tab_close_dialog.rs, dock_menu.rs, global_window.rs (+tests), global_nav.rs (+tests), status_bar.rs, notifications.rs, appearance_sync.rs, metal_layer.rs, theme.rs, symbols.rs, ui_text.rs, chat_tabs.rs, chat.rs, orchestrators.rs, orchestrator_wakes.rs, orchestrator_choice.rs, orchestrator_tab_tests.rs, schedules.rs. All paths are repo-relative. "main.rs" in Notes means `crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs`.

How the GPUI shell works, for orientation:
- One GUI **process** holds any number of project windows (`Workspace`, one per project at a time) and at most one global window.
- Each window keeps a binary split tree (`layouts::Layout`) of panes. Each pane holds an ordered tab list.
- Tabs are Shell, RemoteShell, Panel, Chat or AppPreview.
- Most logic is in `Workspace` and runs off a 2 s per-window `refresh` tick.
- Persistence goes to `layouts.json` (riwork-core `LayoutStore`) and to the shared per-project tab store (`project_tabs::TabStore`, riwork-engine).

### Bridge needs (P-SHELL)
Every module below is registered once per process, so state that is "process-wide" today (claims, caches, pollers) lives in Rust and is shared by every AppView.

- `Cli.run(args) -> bool` [sync, before AppKit]. Wraps `cli::run_cli` (crates/riwork-cli/src/cli.rs:419). Runs once at launch (main.rs:13638).
- `Runtime.register(stateHome)`, `Runtime.restoreFromEnv() -> RestoreSnapshot?`, `Runtime.tryPublishWindows(windows) -> bool`, `Runtime.pendingReload() -> ReloadRequest?`, `Runtime.launchReload(req, windows)`, `Runtime.reloadReady(launch) -> bool`, `Runtime.failReload(req, msg)`, `Runtime.confirmRestore(snapshot, windows)` and `Runtime.noteAttach(id, cwd, phase)`. All [sync]. Wraps crates/riwork-engine/src/runtime.rs:314, :530, :553, :713, :765, :793, :912, :1049, :1614-1620. Polled today by the 500 ms app tick (main.rs:13903-13993).
- `Workspace.resolveStartup(path?, cwd) -> {project, remote?}` [sync]. Wraps `WorkspaceStartup::resolve`/`for_project` (main.rs:1877-1948).
- `Workspace.loadProject(windowId, projectId, carry?) -> {layout, tabs, notice?}` [sync]. A Rust port of `load_project` (main.rs:4040-4413) covering locked-region carry, dropping global and foreign tabs, adopting CLI-made shells, and spawning the first shell. Triggered by window open, project switch, and the next 2 s tick after a failed restore (main.rs:5721).

Wraps crates/riwork-core/src/layouts.rs:1782, :1806, :1813, :1824. Fed today on every structural change, a 250 ms window-size debounce (main.rs:2681), a 250 ms pane-area debounce (main.rs:9858), and quit or release (main.rs:13744, :2270).
- Pure layout ops (port them as Rust calls so the core and main.rs tests keep covering them):

### Host needs (P-SHELL)
- `host.params`: `windowKind` ("project" or "global"), `projectId` (a local id or a `<desktop>:<project>` remote key), `startupPath`, `restore` (a `RuntimeWindow` with frame, mode, layout, focus mode, focus-centred and global state), `startupNotice`, `forcedAppearance` (`RIWORK_APPEARANCE`).
- `host.call("openProjectWindow", {projectId | path})`: ⌘⇧N, Projects "open in new window", Dock "New Window", notification click, and routing from the global window. It must cascade by the open-window count (SHELL-018).
- `host.call("openGlobalWindow", {view, start})` plus `host.on("showView")`: a singleton that also works with no window open.
- `host.call("windows.list")`: frontmost first, including minimised windows and other Spaces, each with kind and projectId. `host.call("routeToProject", {projectId, step})` with `host.on("step")` delivered to the target window (showShell, chat, task, panel, apiKeySettings, projectMemo).
- `host.call("collectRuntimeWindows")`: each AppView returns its `RuntimeWindow` (and saves its layout first when `save`), for the reload snapshot, quit and the registry heartbeat.
- `host.call("quit")`, `host.on("willQuit")` (synchronous per-window save, SHELL-029), `host.on("windowWillClose"/"windowClosed")` (save, release claims, Dock update, quit when the last window closes, SHELL-027).
- `host.on("windowActivated"/"windowDeactivated")`, `host.on("occlusionChanged")` / `host.on("appActive")` (pause sampling and metrics, skip the idle redraw), `host.on("windowFrameChanged")` and `host.on("backingScaleChanged")`.
- `host.call("windowPlacement") -> {mode: windowed|maximized|fullscreen, frame, normalFrame}` and `host.call("displayVisibleFrame")`: needed for a zoomed window's normal frame and the cascade.
- Window chrome: a transparent title bar, traffic lights at (10, 8), a minimum size of 640×400, app-owned drag regions inside content (empty strip space, the 12 pt handle, the controls island), and `titlebarDoubleClick` honouring the system "double-click to zoom/minimise" setting.
- The application menu is owned by the AppKit host and routes `host:command` events. App-level: quit, openSettings, openProjectSettings, openSchedules, openGlobalDashboard, openGlobalChat, biggerText, smallerText, actualSize. Window-level, sent to the key window: newChat, appPreview, sessions, defaultLayout, gatherTabs, toggleTranscriptMode, expandTranscript, collapseTranscript. Menu key equivalents must not steal ⌘=/⌘-/⌘0 from a focused terminal (SHELL-300).
- Dock menu: `host.call("dock.setMenu", items)`, `host.call("dock.updateWindow", {windowId, project, branch})`, `host.on("dock.activateWindow")`, `host.on("dock.newWindow")`.
- Notifications: `host.call("postNotification", {tag, title, body, actions:[{id:"open", title:"Open agent"}]})` and `host.on("notificationResponse", {tag, actionId})`. Requires the app bundle and identity `dev.riwork.shell`.
- `host.call("setAppAppearance")` and `host.call("listFonts")` (to detect SF Mono).
- The `riwork.terminal` native component must support: `command`/`cwd`/`quietLogin`/`themeOverride`/`focusOnSpawn` props; `cmd:focus`, `cmd:paste`, `cmd:updateTheme`, `cmd:reattach`; a `cmd:snapshot`/`frozen` mode or an overlay strategy (SHELL-112); events `attachFailed` and `exited`; backing-scale handling; and claiming ⌘=/⌘-/⌘0 key equivalents while it is first responder.

### Gaps & risks (P-SHELL)
- **Native terminal z-order.** GPUI snapshots and hides every shown terminal while any menu, popover, modal or tab drag is open (SHELL-112), because the Ghostty NSView draws above app content. In NSH, TSX `Overlay`, `Dialog`, `Popover` or `Menu` drawn inside the same AppView will likely sit under the `riwork.terminal` NSView. We need either native NSMenu/NSPopover/child-window overlays or a `riwork.terminal` freeze/snapshot mode: `GAP:overlay above native component`. The notice toast and the drag drop-overlay have the same problem.
- **DockArea vs RiWork's pane model.**
  - RiWork uses a binary split tree with clamped ratios (0.1–0.9), stable numeric pane ids, one optional *main* group, and per-group *locks*. A lock means: no user close, not a gather source, never an adoption or new-tab target, fixed pixel size on window resize, and carried across project switches.
  - RiWork's drop zones are 23% pane edges plus 12 pt window-edge strips, including a top root dock.
  - RiWork needs: append-without-select, keeping the empty main group, a header inset under the traffic lights, and re-keying a chat tab when its id arrives.
  - DockArea (n-ary, left/right/bottom docks, no per-group tab-bar edge, `locked` prop) covers some of this. The rest needs TSX on top or upstream work: `GAP:DockArea locked-group pixel size`, `GAP:DockArea top root dock + custom zone thresholds`, `GAP:DockArea empty main group`, `GAP:DockArea per-group header inset`.
  - The `layouts.json` schema (binary tree) is a cross-build contract and must not change.
- **Process model.** GPUI runs one process with N windows, but there is no single-instance lock: `riwork open` spawns a new process every time. Shell and chat claims are per process, so two processes can show the same shell today. The NSH host must decide between forwarding to a running instance and keeping one process per launch, and the reload/restore protocol (500 ms poll, 20 s confirm, 40 s watchdog) must be reimplemented in the host.
- **Application menu.** The host owns it (coordinator guidance). GPUI has only RiWork, Edit and View menus: no Window or Help menu, no Hide or Minimize. ⌘W closes a tab, never a window. Adding the AppKit defaults changes behaviour, so decide explicitly. Menu key equivalents fire before the focused NSView. ⌘= in a terminal must not also resize UI text (SHELL-300), and ⌘⇧G is Grok globally but Find Previous inside a chat (SHELL-243), which needs `when`-precedence in `nsh/keymap`.
- **Polling.** Today each window reloads settings.json, the store snapshot and session samples every 2 s. On top of that: an app-wide 2 s appearance poll, a 500 ms runtime tick, 1 s schedule, wake and notification pollers, 10 s worktree sync, a 10 s idle redraw, and 15 s CUA checks. Move these into Rust (one per process) with change events, keep the visibility gating (SHELL-030) and the single-flight and generation guards (SHELL-171, SHELL-047, SHELL-227), and do not re-render the window on unchanged ticks.
- **Desktop-only, untested behaviour** that has no Rust test and would only be caught manually:
  - layout menu, notice toast, pane header widths (108/136/152/180 pt), focus-mode geometry, root dock strips, terminal attach retry and freeze/thaw
  - remote strip and Reconnect, Codex usage backoff wiring, `chat_tab_text`/`chat_tab_hint`, `sentence_case`
  - restoring a terminal colour override (SHELL-313d), about 35 notice strings

  Add `nsh-test` coverage while porting.
- **Ownership holes.** subshells.rs (worker watch), handoff_dialog.rs, chat_choice.rs, remote_prompt.rs and nuke_shells.rs appear in no reader's file set. This file has integration rows only (SHELL-326, SHELL-327, SHELL-201 to SHELL-203, SHELL-208).
- **Copy drift.** The tab-close dialog still says "reopen it from ＋ › Open a worker or shell", but the strip no longer has a ＋ (SHELL-145). Fix it before freezing the string as parity.
- **SF Symbols and fonts.** Native uses SF Symbols tinted like text, and SF Mono for accents. If NSH `Image` cannot render system symbols with weight and tint, it needs a native component (SHELL-308).
- **Performance budgets to re-establish:** the busy workspace frame (24 tabs, median and p90 over ≥41 frames; main.rs:15688), the busy dashboard frame (120 projects, 40 shells, 60 chats; crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window/tests.rs:1000), and memory per terminal surface (at most 3 warm hidden terminals, release after 30 s, SHELL-151).

## Terminal, files, remote & previews (P-TERM)

Paths are repo-relative to `/Users/dominik/orca/projects/riWork-nsh-contracts`; `remote/src/…` is the `riwork-remote` crate at the repo root. gpui-libghostty (pinned `=0.3.1`) is cited with its absolute registry path. Tests are `path:line fn_name` (line of the `fn`). Companion contract facts: `facts-terminal.md`.

Key framing: today a terminal tab = **tmux client drawn by an embedded Ghostty surface**. Ghostty does rendering, key encoding, mouse reporting, its own clipboard; tmux owns session, scrollback, copy mode, size; RiWork does titles, cwd, exit, links, file paste/drop **through tmux**, never through Ghostty. The shim forwards no Ghostty action except RENDER.

### Terminal attach/detach & no-recreation of tmux sessions

| ID | Behavior to preserve | GPUI source | Tests | NSH surface | Verify | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### File explorer (tree, git status, watch, open, rename, context menus)

GPUI has **no** git-status decoration, rename/create/delete, context menu, drag-out or filesystem watcher in Files; its whole action surface is the `FileExplorerEvent` enum (crates/riwork-desktop/src/file_explorer.rs:52-73). Adding any of these in NSH is new scope, not parity.

### Bridge needs (P-TERM)

- **Sessions** (crates/riwork-engine/src/sessions.rs): `create(projectId, worktreeId?, cwd, command?)` (:235), `attachCommand(id)` (:1001; must keep the configure-scrolling + hyperlinks side effects :1069-1104), `get/list/sample` (cwds, liveness, :692, :853, :2060), `close(id)` (:2071), `closeMany(ids)` for Nuke (crates/riwork-desktop/src/nuke_shells.rs:228-266), `pasteInput(id, {files[]}|{picture:true})` wrapping `paste` + `terminal_drop::{program,path_input,paste_input}` (:1860; crates/riwork-engine/src/terminal_drop.rs:94-188), `pasteFiles(id, paths)` (:1879), `send/sendKeys` (:1853, :1400), `openFileEditor(...)` (:261), `claim/release(shellId)` process-global (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13105-13124), `nukeScope(projectId)` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/nuke_shells.rs:18).
- **Links** (crates/riwork-engine/src/terminal_links.rs + crates/riwork-desktop/src/terminal_links.rs): `captureView(shellId)` (sessions.rs:1114 + `PaneView::parse` :166), `linkAt({shellId, cell|pointer{x,y,w,h,scale}, cwdFallback, root, home}) -> {link, runs[], grid} | null` (:323, desktop :213-284), `openRefusal(path)` (:1180), `within(root,path)` (:1147). Keep the 500/600/150/400/2000 ms cache in Rust so TSX only forwards pointer events.
- **TerminalLifecycle**: `plan(screen) -> {release[], recheckMs}` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/terminal_lifecycle.rs:98) — pure, move to core/engine.
- **Appearance**: `terminalTheme(settings, systemDark) -> {bg,fg,palette[16]}|null` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/theme.rs:161-216, needs libghostty config read today), `refresh()` with Ghostty config stamps (:254-303), `ghosttyFontSize()` (:515), `ghosttyPadding()` (:548).
- **Runtime**: `noteAttachStarted(id, cwd)`, `noteAttachFinished(id, ok)` (crates/riwork-engine/src/runtime.rs:1614-1624) — the NSH terminal native component should call these itself around surface creation.
- **Subshells**: `captureWorkers`, `poll(state)`, `watchHome(home) -> event`, `dispatch(id, send|stop|open)`, `transitions` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/subshells.rs:228-470; crates/riwork-engine/src/subshells.rs).
- **Remote**: `locateCli`, `attachCommand(desktopId, shellId)`, `tick(wants)` + tree push, `folders/selectedView`, `tabTitle/stripFor/hostName/liveShells/knownShell`, `createShell`, `createProject/validateProjectName`, `orchestrators`, `clickWorktree`, `dismiss*Failure`, `hosts/addHost/removeHost/pairDesktop` (secret link never in JSON logs), `select/selected` (crates/riwork-engine/src/remote_tree.rs, remote_hosts.rs, remote_cache.rs; crates/riwork-desktop/src/remote_service.rs:81-301). State must be process-global (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:5248-5259).
- **Files**: `root`, `listDirectory` (with unchanged flag), `openRefusal`, `revealPlan` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/file_explorer.rs:109-250, 864-1018); `Layouts.planPreviewReveal` (crates/riwork-core/src/layouts.rs:727).
- **FilePreview**: `load`, `renderPdfPage`, `readText/copyBlock`, `loadChatEntry` — must first move out of the GPUI-dependent desktop module into the engine (crates/riwork-desktop/src/file_preview.rs:147-572).
- **AppPreview / PreviewHost / PreviewControl**: `launch/snapshot/setVisible/input/stop/restart/selectWindow/close` (crates/riwork-engine/src/app_preview.rs:115-265); `PreviewHost.open/start→onRequest/respond/remove/instanceId` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/preview_host.rs:13-78; crates/riwork-engine/src/preview_control.rs:154-246); `PreviewControl.begin/beginRun/publish/closed` (:340-547) — preferably a Rust-side 100 ms publisher bound to the handle.

### Host needs (P-TERM)

- `riwork.terminal` native component (NSView hosting libghostty directly): first responder; keyDown/keyUp/flagsChanged with NSEvent.keyCode; mouse down/up/move/drag for L/R/M; scrollWheel (precise flag); `viewDidChangeBackingProperties` (replaces crates/riwork-desktop/src/metal_layer.rs:62-125); main-thread tick on libghostty wakeup; free surface on unmount (detaches tmux client).
- Keymap precedence: NSH keymap must see ⌘T/⌘W/⌘D/⌃Tab/⌘1–9/⌘F/… before the terminal (TERM-027), must NOT take ⌘= ⌘- ⌘0 ⌘C ⌘A in a terminal (TERM-028/037), and must offer ⌘V to RiWork first with a "propagate to Ghostty" fallback (TERM-039). Edit menu must not double-dispatch (TERM-029).
- Mouse interception: a way to swallow a ⌘-click (press + matching release) before Ghostty, decided from a link answer computed ahead of time (TERM-055).
- Z-order: TSX overlays (menus, Popover, Tooltip, Dialog, drop outline, link underline) above native terminal views — or keep snapshot+hide (TERM-071) and CALayer overlays at zPosition 1000 (TERM-050).
- `onFileDrop` delivering Finder file URLs with drag-over position over a terminal (TERM-046); `nsh/clipboard` reading file URLs, images and text types separately (TERM-039).
- `host:windowState` (visible, key/active) for subshell pause, app preview gating, release policy (TERM-079, TERM-177).
- `host:appearance` change notification (macOS light/dark) (TERM-068).
- `host:revealInFinder`, `host:openWithSystem` (after Rust `openRefusal`), `host:sharePicker` (TERM-139, TERM-062, TERM-165); `nsh/device openUrl` for http/https/mailto only (TERM-060).
- Pinch/magnify and ⌃-scroll gestures for the file preview (TERM-162).
- A native `riwork.preview` frame view (CGImage/CALayer, aspect-fit, mouse capture incl. up-outside, NSTextInputClient) for app preview (TERM-183–188).
- Background executor for blocking remote/tmux calls (5–90 s) off the JS/main thread (TERM-107).
- Window title setter for remote projects (TERM-109).

### Gaps & risks (P-TERM)

- **GAP: no libghostty-without-GPUI embedding.** The 0.3.1 shim takes the GPUI window's NSView as parent and its child view refuses hit-testing (/Users/dominik/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/gpui-libghostty-0.3.1/shim/ghostty_surface.m:36-41, 177-270). `riwork.terminal` needs its own shim/fork that owns input. Biggest spike item.
- **GAP: reparenting.** GPUI moves a live terminal between panes (TERM-014); if NSH remounts native components on parent change, every move re-attaches (tmux repaints, but Ghostty font zoom/selection are lost).
- **GAP: z-order.** Native views over TSX is the reason for snapshot freezing and CALayer overlays (TERM-050, TERM-071, TERM-087, TERM-095). Must be settled before menus/dialogs are built.
- **GAP: exit and respawn.** No push exit event today (2 s poll) and no in-place respawn (TERM-067, TERM-075, TERM-096) — contract needs `event:exit` and `cmd:respawn` (or keyed remount).
- **GAP: ⌘-click interception and ⌘V pre-check** need synchronous host hooks (TERM-039, TERM-055).
- **Decision: IME** (none today, TERM-025), **momentum scroll** (TERM-033), **TERM value** (TERM-006/113), **clipboard policy** (deny-all, TERM-036), **remote `--ignore-size`** (TERM-023). Each is a behavior change if altered.
- **Risk: link geometry reverse-engineering.** RiWork recomputes Ghostty's cell grid from config padding and tmux client size (TERM-052); any Ghostty version/config drift breaks underline placement. A direct libghostty integration could expose cell size instead.
- **Risk: link polling cost.** Control-mode watch is disabled, so a resting pointer costs a tmux capture every ~600 ms (TERM-054).
- **Risk: frame/bitmap transport.** App preview frames (≤16 MiB @100 ms) and file preview images (~13 MB BGRA) must not go through the JSON bridge as base64 (TERM-158, TERM-183).
- **Risk: GPUI-only logic.** File preview load/decode lives in the desktop crate with GPUI types; launcher-extension lists are duplicated (crates/riwork-desktop/src/file_explorer.rs:969 vs crates/riwork-engine/src/terminal_links.rs:1157).
- **Risk: secrets.** Pairing links must never enter props/events/logs (TERM-114/115).
- **Test gaps**: no tests for attach retry/placeholder (TERM-010), theme replacement (TERM-067), remote strip/reconnect (TERM-095/096), drop outline (TERM-046), terminal zoom passthrough (TERM-028), explorer keyboard/visuals (TERM-133/137), `preview_request` (TERM-203); shell-paste CLI tests are `#[ignore]`.
- **Not present in GPUI (do not treat as parity)**: Files git status / rename / context menu / drag-out / FS watch; terminal title, bell, OSC 7, OSC 9/777, find-in-terminal, clear/reset commands, drag from Files into a terminal.

## Chat transcript (P-CHATT)

Scope: the reading side of `crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view` (transcript model, streaming, projection, virtual list, row and part rendering, links/references, notices, workers, selection, find, navigation, review, file actions, feedback, perf). The composer/input side (panels.rs composer, editors, suggest, attachments, dictate, memo, toolbar, drafts) is covered by another reader. Where a row cites `panels.rs`, it is only for the transcript list's own chrome. Line numbers refer to branch `nsh-contracts` @ `50b3b41`.

Rule sources cited in Notes: `docs/chat-events.md`, `docs/chat-notices.md`, `docs/tool-ui-performance.md`, `spec/transcript-grouping/v2/README.md` ("spec"), `GPUI_KIT_TRANSCRIPT_SELECTION_*.md`, `inline-timeline-design-20261014.md`, `PERFORMANCE_RECOVERY_REPORT.txt`.

Architecture assumption for every row: the event fold (`Transcript`), the grouping projection (`riwork-engine chat/projection.rs`), the Markdown parse, highlighting, diff classification and selection document all stay in Rust behind `riwork/native:Chat` (plus `Markdown`, `Diff`, `Diagram`, `Links`, `Files` bridges). The bridge pushes row patches to a TSX `List`. TSX owns layout, hover, focus and gestures only.

### Transcript model, transport & streaming

| ID | Behavior to preserve | GPUI source | Tests | NSH surface | Verify | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CHATT-001 | A chat tab subscribes to the host's event log by chat id from `since=0`. It replays the whole history, then follows live. Closing the tab, reloading the app or restarting the host never loses the chat. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:482; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:258; crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/client.rs:509 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:611 a_subscription_replays_what_happened_and_then_follows_the_chat | `riwork/native:Chat.subscribe(chatId, since)` → event stream of row patches | bridge | The subscription runs on a Rust thread. It must not run in JS. |
| CHATT-002 | Gapless sequence. An event with `seq <= last` is a Repeat and is skipped. `last+1` is Next. Anything higher is a Gap: the connection is dropped and the tab subscribes again from the last seq it handed over. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:127-133, :330-337 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:602 events_are_next_repeated_or_missing; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:628 after_the_host_drops_the_connection_it_subscribes_again_from_the_last_event | `riwork/native:Chat.subscribe` (internal) | bridge | docs/chat-events.md:3-6. Elided events still take up a sequence slot. |
| CHATT-003 | The model fold ignores any envelope whose seq is at or before `last_seq`, so a replay after reconnect changes nothing. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:98-108 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:346 an_event_seen_before_changes_nothing | `riwork/native:Chat` fold | bridge | |
| CHATT-004 | Reconnect backoff: the first retry waits 500 ms and the wait doubles with each consecutive failure, capped at 5 s. A connection that delivered anything resets the failure count to 1. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:103-113, :282-289 | none for the exact numbers (tests use 10/40 ms at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:554) | `riwork/native:Chat.subscribe` | bridge | Add a bridge unit test for 500 ms / 5 s. |
| CHATT-005 | The link state moves Connecting → Live → Reconnecting → Deleted. A chat the host does not list counts as Deleted only after 2 consecutive misses, because a host that just started may not have loaded it yet. Events arriving clear the local Link notice. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:14-23; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:270-281; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:899-918 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:650 a_chat_the_host_does_not_list_any_more_ends_the_feed_as_deleted | `riwork/native:Chat.onLink(chatId)` event | bridge | |
| CHATT-006 | An event this build cannot decode (from a newer host) becomes a Warning notice item with id `unreadable-<seq>` and the text "An event from the chat host could not be read. This RiWork may be older than the host." The stream continues after it. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:325-327, :355-376 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:673 an_event_this_build_cannot_read_is_skipped_with_a_note_and_the_rest_follow | bridge | bridge | |
| CHATT-007 | Batching: the UI pump drains every message waiting in the channel into one batch, which is applied and redrawn once. It then waits 16 ms before taking the next batch. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:876-891 | none (structural) | `riwork/native:Chat.subscribe` coalesces into ≤1 patch per frame (~16 ms) | bridge | This is the key streaming-performance contract. Never send one JS message per token delta. |
| CHATT-008 | Fold semantics. `ItemStarted` and `ItemCompleted` replace an item by id and keep its first-arrival position. `ItemDelta` appends Text to AgentMessage/Reasoning and Output to Command. `TurnCompleted` closes every InProgress item and marks those rows touched. | crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/model.rs:1259, :1293, :1320; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:120-153 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:305 new_items_are_appended_and_changed_ones_are_touched_by_position | `riwork/native:Chat` fold (engine `Transcript::apply_sequenced`) | core | inline-timeline-design-20261014.md:37-38. |
| CHATT-009 | Each batch reports `appended`, `touched` (positions of pre-existing items only), `events`, and `projection_changed`. `projection_changed` is set only by ItemStarted, ItemCompleted, TurnStarted, TurnCompleted, Approval*/Question* and State. Token deltas never reproject the history. | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:27-35, :109-119, :155-167 | crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/state.rs:305 new_items_are_appended_and_changed_ones_are_touched_by_position; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/display_tests.rs:346 large_disclosed_turn_virtualizes_children_and_streams_within_budget (asserts "tokens reprojected historical calls" at :468) | bridge patch types `append / update(rowIds) / reproject` | bridge | |

### Bridge needs (P-CHATT)

Rules that apply to every bridge:
- **Coalesce.** Push at most one patch per ~16 ms per chat. Today: the feed thread feeds an `async_channel` that the UI drains once per frame, then waits 16 ms (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:876-891).
- **Never send per-token JS messages.**
- **Offsets.** Every text range in Rust is a UTF-8 byte offset. The bridge must convert to UTF-16 (NSH uses UTF-16 everywhere, per nsh-core src/selection.rs).
- **Clock.** Timers need an injectable clock for nsh-test (`TEST_NOW`, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/notices.rs:22).

**`riwork/native:Chat`** wraps the engine `Transcript` fold and the projection. Today's trigger: the per-chat follow thread `Subscription::open` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/client.rs:509), then the channel, then `accept`/`sync_list` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:895, :954).

### Host needs (P-CHATT)

**Preview and files**
- `host:openPreview(sequence, imageScope)`: selects the chat's worktree in the Files pane and shows the preview sequence. Window-level guards: "This chat's project is not open in this window." and "This chat's worktree is unavailable." (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:8574-8590).
- `host:openFileReadOnly(target)` for global-orchestrator chats (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:8327).
- `host:openFile` / Open in Files.
- `host:revealInFinder`, `host:openWithSystem` (file_actions, image "Open original").
- AppKit Quick Look for sent-attachment clicks (docs/chat-events.md:195).

**Dialogs**
- `host:savePanel(dir, suggestedName)` plus Warning alerts: "Replace the existing file?", "Replace the existing file with this plan?", "Close this edited snippet?". Use these if `nsh/base:Dialog` is not window-modal.

**Window routing**
- `host:listShellTabs` / `host:focusShellTab(shellId)` for "Copy & open terminal…". The window sets the notice "Snippet copied…". The global window passes an empty list.
- `host:openWorkerShell` / `host:openWorkerChat` (another tab or window).
- `host:showUsagePanel`, `host:openApiKeySettings`, `host:editProjectMemo`.
- `host:handOff` / `host:newChat` (ChatViewEvent variants at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:118-171).
- `host:tabSummaryChanged`: the window re-reads `summary()` to title tabs and count agents (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1598-1604).
- Tab-visibility signal for worker summaries and deferred feeds (crates/riwork-desktop/src/subshells.rs:740; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:538).

**Menus and keys**
- Edit ▸ Copy routed to the transcript Copy action (crates/riwork-desktop/src/behavior_controls.rs:162-174). ⌘C stays unbound for terminal and AppPreview.
- The window keymap fall-through: ⌘F is window search outside the chat; ⌘⇧G is Open Grok while Find is closed (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13805-13828).

**Environment**
- Appearance (light/dark, theme choice) and text-scale change events. These drive syntect and Mermaid themes and remeasure all rows.
- Bundling the signed `riwork-diagram` helper beside the app binary.
- An NSPasteboard PNG write, if `nsh/clipboard` image accepts only file/URI input.

### Gaps & risks (P-CHATT)

1. **Cross-row selection does not fit NSH 0.3's selection model.** This is the biggest risk. NSH selects across the texts of a `selectable` View: a point is (text index in the current layout, UTF-16 offset), and copy takes the laid-out text (nsh-core 0.3 src/selection.rs:1-40, src/ops.rs:2139-2143). RiWork needs:
   - endpoints keyed by stable leaf key and UTF-8 byte that survive recycled/unmounted List cells (CHATT-238);
   - copy exported from the Rust source projection, keeping whitespace and code indentation with exactly one `\n` between leaves (CHATT-236/237);
   - forced clearing before paint while streaming (CHATT-242);
   - a link-click veto while selecting (CHATT-173/247);
   - one selection per window.
   
   Expect `GAP:keyed virtual selection`: a native selection layer over the List, or an NSH extension that reports `{elementKey, utf16Offset}` endpoints and lets the app supply the copy text. Several fixtures named in the selection reports no longer exist (multi-click, Shift-extend, ⌘C focus priority, link veto). The two virtualization fixtures are `#[ignore]`.
2. **Find needs primitives NSH lacks.** It needs TextView range highlights (match vs. current-match styles), a rect query for a text range, and programmatic offsets for nested horizontal `Scroll`s, so it can place a match 30% from the top inside code/table/diff boxes (CHATT-264/265). None of these are documented in NSH 0.3.
3. **Virtual list contract.** Variable-height rows with targeted remeasure of single items, `splice` that keeps measurements, anchor-by-stable-id restore across reprojection and section splits (CHATT-038/039/042/044), and a follow-tail mode that RiWork can pause programmatically. NSH List covers `scrollToIndex`, `maintainVisibleContentPosition` (follow end) and `onViewableItemsChange`. Exact-pixel anchor restore after a disclosure toggle, and per-item remeasure, still need verification.
4. **Performance budgets now include bridge serialization.** Budgets: <1.5 ms per event, <25 ms per frame, <60 ms per toggle, <100 rows built per frame. Today's 25 ms frame guard already fails in Debug (26.7–36 ms, PERFORMANCE_RECOVERY_REPORT.txt:247-252). Set explicit NSH budgets and keep the 2,200-call fixture as a shared perf test. Line-level virtualization of one huge message remains deferred.
5. **The Markdown parser must stay Rust.** Feed `nsh/base:TextView` RiWork's own blocks; never raw Markdown. nsh's markdown-rs differs in several ways:
   - a single newline becomes a space;
   - nesting depth is 32 instead of 8;
   - no Codex citations;
   - different loose-list nesting;
   - no bare-image-path cards;
   - no streaming-prefix guarantee.
   
   Highlighting also differs: nsh syntax uses fixed token colors and jsx→tsx aliasing, with no title fallback and no size limits. Pass RiWork's syntect spans for color parity.
6. **DiffView.** Turn off word diff and collapsed context, and keep the 400-line cap, RiWork line kinds, the theme diff colors and complete-patch copy. Or record these as accepted visual changes.
7. **HeaderLine and meter have no NSH equivalent.** HeaderLine needs per-run fonts with an ellipsis that never leaves "·…" and hides when even "…" does not fit (CHATT-208). The usage meter needs measured text widths to drop cost, then counts (CHATT-213). Both need a text-measure bridge or a native label. Neither has tests.
8. **Nested scroll and wheel propagation.** Output, code, table and command boxes inside the virtual list must lock to one axis and pass the wheel on (or not) exactly as today (CHATT-138, CHATT-142). Possible `GAP:scroll wheel propagation control`. Image viewer zoom and pan possibly need `GAP:zoomable Scroll` (CHATT-157).
9. **Bitmap and memory bounds.** Today: 8 decoded images, 16 opened cards, 64 thumbnails at 360 px, 4 bitmaps per diagram, and dropping late results by stale generation (CHATT-154, CHATT-103). NSH `Image` needs Rust-owned handles so that NSImage caching cannot exceed these bounds.
10. **Thread and time assumptions.** Highlight and Markdown caches are per-thread or per-item. Timers (activity clock 1 s, copied state 1.5 s, notice expiry, worker starting grace 30 s, summaries 2 s settle / 30 s interval) need an injectable clock in bridge tests. AI worker summaries cost money, so keep the visible-tab gate.
11. **Test gaps to close before porting.** None of these have tests today:
    - reconnect backoff numbers (CHATT-004);
    - row keyboard focus ↑/↓/Space (CHATT-032);
    - activity clock (CHATT-034);
    - footer and creation states (CHATT-017/019);
    - the Copy-message button (CHATT-054);
    - card-head rules (CHATT-116–123);
    - image-label rules (CHATT-152);
    - autolinks and bare-URL trimming (CHATT-084);
    - the diff "… N more lines" footer;
    - HeaderLine.
12. **Doc/code drift and dead code.**
    - docs/chat-notices.md:113 shows "resets Tue 14:00"; banners actually render "resets Tue at 14:00".
    - select.rs still refreshes `header:` leaves that nothing emits.
    - Edit ▸ Select All has no transcript handler, so its intended behavior is undefined.
    - The Escape priority chain (CHATT-267) spans the composer reader's `editors.rs`. The two parity lists must agree on it.
13. **Not covered here (by design).** The composer, editors, suggestions, attachments (except how sent ones render), dictation, memo, toolbar and drafts belong to the composer reader. The question panel and approval-bar rendering live in `panels.rs` and belong to that reader too; this list covers only the approval key and decision rules. Relay elision UI applies to the phone only (CHATT-021). There is no video or audio in the transcript.

## Chat composer & chat chrome (P-CHATC)

Scope: the composer and the chrome around it in `crates/riwork-desktop/src`: chat_view/{panels,composer,editors,suggest_ui,attachment_ui(+native_paste,quick_look,sent),attachment_draft,image_scope,dictate,memo,toolbar}.rs; chat_drafts(+references).rs, chat_choice.rs, delegation_choice.rs, dictation.rs, handoff_dialog.rs, memo_editor.rs, chat_tabs.rs, text_input.rs, form_input.rs. Rules are taken from GPUI_KIT_INPUT_CONTRACT.md, GPUI_KIT_DRAFT_IMAGE_PREVIEW_RECEIPT.md, GPUI_KIT_FORMS_PLAN.md/RECEIPT.md, docs/chat-delegation.md and docs/subscription-balancing.md. Engine calls are followed only far enough to name where the behavior lives.
Conventions: all paths are repo-relative. A `path:a-b` cite is the line range of the function or block. Tests are given as `path:line fn_name`. "none" means there is no automated test today. Rows that belong to the transcript side (image cards, the usage chip, notice history) are left to the transcript reader, and only their composer-side entry points appear here.

### Bridge needs (P-CHATC)

Format: `Bridge.fn(args) -> result` [sync|async|event], the wrapped core or engine API, and what triggers a refresh today.

- **Chat (send, keys, receipts)**
  - `Chat.submit(chatId, submissionId, snapshot{text, references, attachments}, editorGeneration) -> {status: "submitted"|"refused"|"uncertain", error?}` [async, exactly once]. Wraps the snapshot command built at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_draft.rs:64-84, then `Feed::submit` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:212-219) → `Client::command_checked` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/client.rs:336). Must do the durable recovery write first (`chat_drafts::begin_submission`, called at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:571-582) inside the same call. Today the receipt comes back over the feed's `async_channel` as `FeedMsg::Submission` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:407-412 → crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:919-925). It must never auto-retry.
  - `Chat.onSubmissionReceipt(chatId, submissionId, result)` [event]. Replaces the `FeedMsg::Submission` channel (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:919-925). TSX clears the composer only when its editor generation still matches (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_draft.rs:85-101).
  - `Chat.interrupt(chatId) -> void` [async, fire-and-forget, ordered]. Wraps `ChatCommand::Interrupt` through `Feed::send` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:205-210); the engine side is crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/host.rs:2648-2654. It is triggered by Cmd+. or the Stop button, and only when the state is Running/Waiting (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2179-2182).
  - `Chat.stop(chatId) -> void` [async]. Wraps `ChatCommand::Stop` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:779-783 → crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/host.rs:2621-2624).
  - `Chat.approve(chatId, requestId, decision) -> void` [async]. Wraps `ChatCommand::Approve` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/model.rs:979-982), called today at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:219-223 and :473-477. Command failure comes back as `FeedMsg::CommandFailed` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:935) and un-marks the request as answered (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1635-1639).
  - `Chat.answer(chatId, submissionId, requestId, answers: string[][]) -> receipt` [async, exactly once]. Wraps `ChatCommand::Answer` via `Feed::submit` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:968-1008), with the receipt delivered through the same Submission event.
  - `Chat.state(chatId) -> {chatId?, link: Live|Deleted, state: Idle|Running|Waiting|..., approvals[], questions[], models[]}` [event stream]. Drives composer disabled (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2311-2313), Stop visibility (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:2907-2918), approval keys (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2196-2204) and the model placeholder (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:232-245). Today it is the feed follower thread pushing `FeedMsg` events into the view (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:385-389, consumed at crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:905-935).
- **Chat chrome (toolbar, selectors, new chat, handoff)**
  - `Chat.configure({model?, effort?, approvalMode?, fast?, contributorMode?, claudeBilling?})` [async, returns a receipt]: wraps `ChatCommand::Configure` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1779-1799, 1826-1906). `Chat.switchProvider({provider, model?})` wraps `ChatCommand::Switch` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1727-1742). `Chat.compact()`, `Chat.stop()`, `Chat.resume()` and `Chat.approve({requestId, decision})` wrap Compact / Stop / an empty Configure / Approve (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:778-783, 1769-1777, 1982-1990). `Chat.delete()` [async] wraps crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1992. Today all of them go through `feed::send` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/feed.rs:206). Chat info, models and account come back through the chat feed stream.
  - `Chat.onInfo -> {provider, model, effort, fast, approvalMode, contributorMode, claudeBilling, usage, account{apiKeySource}, threadId, state}` [event]: the transcript `info`/`models`/`account`/`usage` that the feed pushes today.
  - `Toolbar.describe(chatId) -> {modelLabel, modelRows, effortRows, effortAvailable, fastAvailable, billing{label,short,hint,on,mismatch,click}, apiKeyBadge?, usage{percent, text, details, tone, fraction}}` [sync]: keeps the pure helpers in crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/toolbar.rs:26-452 in Rust (or ports them to TS and moves the 9 unit tests).
  - `Catalog.saved(projectId) -> {codex, claude, muse: {supported[], configured[], account?, balanced, error?}}` [async]: wraps `chat::catalog::saved/available` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_choice.rs:139-150). Reads saved metadata only and never starts a host.
  - `Chat.create(NewChat) -> chatId` [async]: wraps `Client::create` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:789-801), with the chat-tab reservation.
  - `Handoff.start(request) -> taskId` [async] + `Handoff.onProgress(text) / onDone(outcome) / onFailed(error)` [event]: wraps `handoff::run(env, request, progress)` (crates/riwork-engine/src/handoff.rs:330). Today it runs on a background executor with a progress channel (crates/riwork-desktop/src/handoff_dialog.rs:426-480).
  - `Accounts.cachedCodex() -> [{id, label, available, isSystemDefault}]` [sync]: wraps `codex_accounts::cached_accounts` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/handoff_dialog.rs:152-169). Today it is read when the dialog opens. The quota cache is fed by desktop refreshes (docs/subscription-balancing.md:28-41).
  - `Settings.set({chatDisplay})`, `Settings.delegationPolicy` get/set [sync]: wraps `SettingsStore::update` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:1249-1259) and `delegation_choice::settings_policy` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/delegation_choice.rs:30).
- **Drafts, suggestions, memo**
  - `Drafts.init(dataDir) -> void` [sync, once at startup]: wraps `Drafts::load` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:174) and `init` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:707). Today it runs once from app start (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13718). The store must leave riwork-desktop because it is a gpui `Global` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:704).
  - `Drafts.recover(chatId) -> {draft:{text,attachments,references}, submissions[], warnings[]} | null` [sync]: wraps `recover` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:433). Today it is called on `ChatView::open` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:490) through `restore_draft` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:113).
  - `Drafts.save(chatId, draft, owner:{editor,generation}, unready) -> void` [sync enqueue; the native writer thread persists]: wraps `remember_complete` / `set_draft_with_unready` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:727, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:259). Today it fires on every composer `InputEvent::Change` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:182) and on every attachment/reference mutation (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:516, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/references.rs:147).
  - `Drafts.owns(chatId, owner) -> bool` [sync]: wraps `owns` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:745). It is used before re-saving after a refused send (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:597).
  - `Drafts.beginSubmission(chatId, owner, saved) -> Promise<void>` [async; resolves once the snapshot is fsynced]: wraps `begin_submission` plus its ack barrier (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:331, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:522). It is better folded into `Chat.send` so the barrier stays native (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:575, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:618).
  - `Drafts.settleSubmission(chatId, key, status)` / `Drafts.dismissSubmission(chatId, key)` / `Drafts.pendingSubmission(chatId, key) -> bool` [sync]: wraps crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:404, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:426, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:376. Today these are triggered by the submission receipt (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:712) and the Dismiss button (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:809).
  - `Drafts.forget(chatId)` [sync]: wraps crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:323. Today it is triggered by feed `Link::Deleted` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:911) and a delete success (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2012). It is better done natively on the feed's Deleted event.
  - `Drafts.clearSent(chatId, sentText)` [sync]: wraps crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:309. This is the legacy text receipt path.
  - `Drafts.flush(timeoutMs=5000) -> Result` [sync, bounded]: wraps `flush_pending` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:390, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:774). Today it is triggered by an explicit app reload (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13975).
  - `Drafts.shutdown()` [sync, blocking join]: wraps `finish` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:457). Today it is triggered by `cx.on_app_quit` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:709).
  - `Attachments.validateRecovered(chatId, attachment) -> Result` [async]: wraps `validate_attachment` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:633) and `recovery_directory` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:798). Today it runs from restore on the background executor (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:333).
  - `Suggest.trigger(text, caretByte) -> Trigger | null` [sync] and `Suggest.accept(text, trigger, insert) -> {text, caret}` [sync]: wrap crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:111 and crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:149. Today they run on every composer change/caret/focus observe (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:594) and on each render (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2351).
  - `Suggest.commands(provider) -> Command[]` [sync] and `Suggest.rankCommands(query, provider) -> Command[]` [sync]: wrap crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:237 and crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:254.
  - `Suggest.files(root, query) -> {entries[≤50], truncated, loading}` [async, with a native 30 s cache shared by tabs] plus the event `Suggest.onListing(root)`: wraps `list_files` / `rank_files` / `FileCache` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:560, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:748, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:784). Today the cache is a gpui Global with a waiter list (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:357).
  - `Suggest.peek(root, path) -> {kind:'text',lines[]} | 'binary' | 'unreadable'` [async]: wraps crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:974. Today it is triggered by a highlight change (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:509).
  - `Suggest.models(chatId, query) -> {entries[], current, loading}` [async, with a native 30 s cache keyed by (dataDir, projectId)] plus the event `Suggest.onModels`: wraps `log::read_infos`, `catalog::mention_lists`, `ModelUsage::of`, `mention_entries`, `rank_models`, `current_model` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:350, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:423, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:467, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:480). Today it reads off-thread (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:412). It must re-rank when the chat's model or its driver's model list changes (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:234).
  - `Suggest.preview(item, provider, peek?) -> {title, kind, body[], inserts, effect}` [sync]: wraps crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:825, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:880, crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:909. Alternatively, reuse `Suggest.reply(chatId)` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/suggest.rs:1065), which the phone already consumes.
  - `Memo.commandRest(draft) -> string | null` and `Memo.appendToDraft(draft, memo) -> string` [sync]: wrap crates/riwork-core/src/project_memo.rs:90 and crates/riwork-core/src/project_memo.rs:103. Today they are triggered by Send (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:503).
  - `Memo.get(projectId) -> {text, updatedAt} | error` [sync/async]: wraps `Store::snapshot` + `State::project_memo` (crates/riwork-core/src/store.rs:342). Today it is read when `/memo` opens the preview (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/memo.rs:33).
  - `Memo.save(projectId, text, generation) -> {memo} | {error}` [async; native drops a save older than the newest written]: wraps `Store::set_project_memo` (crates/riwork-core/src/store.rs:1511) plus the generation guard (crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:84). Today it is triggered by the 600 ms debounce, blur, editor drop and ⌘S (crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:25, crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:126, crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:330, crates/riwork-desktop/src/project_settings.rs:1361).
  - `Store.onChange(state)` [event]: replaces the per-tick re-read in Project Settings (crates/riwork-desktop/src/project_settings.rs:981), which feeds `MemoEditor::refresh` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:262).
- **Attachments, sent media, image scope**
  - `Attachments.stage(chatId, paths[]) -> chipIds[]` [async] + `Attachments.onChipChange -> {chatId, chips:[{id,name,state,size,previewUrl?,error?}]}` [event]: wraps `stage_source` -> `Client::stage_attachment` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/client.rs:188) -> `attachments::stage` (crates/riwork-engine/src/chat/attachments.rs:241). Today each attempt is a background task whose completion is applied by chip UUID (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:585-634).
  - `Attachments.stageClipboard(chatId) -> chipIds[] | {error}` [async]: native pasteboard read (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/native_paste.rs:19-172) + `clipboard_sources` precedence (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:153) + raw-image admission (:192). The image bytes stay native.
  - `Attachments.retry(chipId)`, `Attachments.remove(chipId)`, `Attachments.open(chipId) -> {quickLook:true} | {inline:true}` [sync]: crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:635-735.
  - `Media.sentThumbnail(chatId, attachmentId) -> fileUrl | {error}` [async]: guarded by `ImageScope::snapshot` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/image_scope.rs:154) and `own_snapshot` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/sent.rs:58).
  - `ImageScope.resolve(chatId, target) -> {root, path} | {error}` [sync] and `ImageScope.onChange(chatId)` [event]: `ImageScope::for_chat` / `resolve` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/image_scope.rs:84, 177). Today it is recomputed when the store state or the chat list changes (global window `refresh_image_scope`, crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window/tests.rs:276).
- **Dictation, settings, chat-tab ownership**
  - `Dictation.start({chatId}) -> {session}` [async]: builds `Sources::of_chat` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/dictation.rs:505), spawns `Engine::start` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/dictation.rs:641), takes the app-wide single owner (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:266-282). Today the helper's stdout reader thread feeds an `async_channel` that is pumped per session (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:295-322).
  - `Dictation.stop({sendAfter}) / cancel() / dismiss() / userEdited({text, selection})` [sync]: drive `Machine::handle` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/dictation.rs:198) and the `Insertion` snapshots (crates/riwork-desktop/src/dictation.rs:358-470). Return the composer edit `{text, selection}` with UTF-16 offsets.
  - `Dictation.onEvent -> {session, phase, note?, edit?{text, selection}, deliver?, failed?{message, settingsUrl}, sendNow?}` [event]: carries every phase change. The 6 s finish deadline runs in Rust (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:195-200).
  - `Settings.get().dictationMic` / `Settings.onChange` [sync + event]: wraps `Settings.dictation_mic` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/settings.rs:244). Today `cx.observe_global::<Settings>` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:571-575) notifies every open chat.
  - `ChatTabs.claim(chatId, explicit) -> bool`, `release(chatId)`, `releaseAll(windowId)`, `reserve(projectId) -> token`, `finishReservation(token, chatId)`, `dropReservation(token)`, `epoch() -> u64` [sync]: wraps `chat_tabs::Coordinator` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_tabs.rs:21-107). These are synchronous mutex calls today, made on tab open/close and window close (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:6370, 6520; crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:1156-1173).

### Host needs (P-CHATC)

- `host:keyEvents`: if NSH's TSX key events don't carry it, the host must report `isRepeat` and key-up for Enter, Escape and the arrows inside the chat view. The held-key guards depend on both (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2387-2399, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:379-384), and so does the focus-out reset (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:600-608).
- `host:inputComposition`: if NSH `Input` does not expose it to TSX, the host needs to report NSTextView `hasMarkedText` for the focused composer, model field, answer fields and find field. Enter, Escape and Tab guards and menu key ownership depend on it (crates/riwork-desktop/src/text_input.rs:97-148, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:266-286).
- `host:appWillTerminate`: a blocking quit hook that calls `Drafts.shutdown()`. It drains the newest draft per chat and gives up to 3 last retries 250 ms apart (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:546, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:709). The same hook flushes any memo save still pending (the GPUI `MemoEditor` Drop, crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:330).
- `host:reload`: an explicit app reload must call `Drafts.flush(5000)` first and cancel the reload if it fails (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13975).
- `host:openProjectMemo(projectId)`: the memo preview's Edit has to reach Project Settings, which may live in another window (project window or global window route) (crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:8610, crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:1328).
- Multi-window: draft ownership (editor id + generation) and the deleted-chat set are process-global (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:134, crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:142). If NSH runs each AppKit window's TSX in its own JS context, the store must stay one native singleton per process.
- `host:openPanel({files:true, directories:false, multiple:true, prompt:"Attach UTF-8 text, PNG or JPEG"}) -> paths[] | null` for "Files and images…" (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:389-394).
- `host:quickLook.show({path, displayName, uti}) / close()`: today this is a `qlmanage -p -c <uti>` child process plus NSRunningApplication activation (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/quick_look.rs:203-275). In an NSH AppKit host an in-process QLPreviewPanel is the better fit. Keep the "one panel; the same file is reused, another file replaces it" rule and the private-copy cleanup, plus the 1 h sweep at start.
- Typed NSPasteboard read (file URLs, NSFilenamesPboardType, UTType conformance, PNG > JPEG > TIFF with byte caps): either the Rust native module (objc2, as today) or a host call. `nsh/clipboard` readImage/readFiles is likely too coarse.
- File drag-over highlight on the composer pane needs drag-enter/leave events, in addition to the drop itself.
- `host:openSettings({section:"apiKey"})`: the billing switch's EnterKey click emits `OpenApiKeySettings` to the window (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:1890). The Settings window or panel is another window.
- `host:newChatChooser`: "New chat…" in the model menu emits `ChatViewEvent::NewChat` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:2387-2392). The workspace hosts the chooser dialog and the pane placement (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_choice.rs:36-48).
- Handoff "Done" opens a new shell or chat tab in the window (`HandoffEvent::Done`, crates/riwork-desktop/src/handoff_dialog.rs:40). A detached dialog reports progress to the window, which outlives the dialog.
- Microphone and Speech Recognition TCC prompts come from the `riwork-speech` helper process. The NSH .app must embed the helper in Contents/MacOS and carry `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` / `NSSpeechRecognitionUsageDescription` (bundle config, not a runtime call).
- "Open System Settings" uses `x-apple.systempreferences:` URLs (crates/riwork-desktop/src/dictation.rs:90-100). `nsh/device` openUrl should be enough. If NSH restricts URL schemes, add a `host:openSystemSettings(pane)` call.

### Gaps & risks (P-CHATC)

**GAPs against NSH 0.3.** These are things that neither `Input` nor `nsh/base:Editor`/keymap is known to offer. Confirm each against nsh-base 0.3.0 before treating it as final.
- `GAP:input-key-intercept`: the chat screen must decide Return, ↑/↓, Tab and Escape before the field acts. Return can mean send, approve, choose a menu row, accept a suggestion, or do nothing (held key). Shift+Return and Alt+Return must still insert a newline. Today this is Kit's InputEvent::PressEnter plus capture-phase actions (crates/riwork-desktop/src/text_input.rs:30-33, 225-238; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2400-2410; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:599-651). It blocks the core send gesture.
- `GAP:input-marked-text-state`: Enter, Escape and Tab must be left to an IME that is composing, and suggestions are suppressed while it composes. The new-chat and handoff dialogs ignore Escape while composing (crates/riwork-desktop/src/text_input.rs:97-148; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:214-220; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_choice.rs:699-711; crates/riwork-desktop/src/handoff_dialog.rs:532-536). Without a composing flag, CJK users send half-composed text.
- `GAP:input-paste-intercept`: a cancellable pre-insert paste hook. Composer ⌘V turns files and images into attachment chips (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:3148-3166), and form fields fold newlines (crates/riwork-desktop/src/form_input.rs:63-89).
- `GAP:key-repeat-and-keyup`: held-key guards (held ⏎ through a menu opening, a held ↓ moving once, no approval on a repeat, key-up re-arming) need `isRepeat` and key-up from `nsh/keymap` or from Input key events (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:333-339, 379-384; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:2387-2399).
- `GAP:input-programmatic-edit` (only if neither Input nor Editor offers it): programmatic set of value and selection and a ranged replace that is one undo step, without being reported as a user edit. Suggestion accept, dictation streaming, memo insert, draft restore and submission restore all depend on it (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:690-718; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:251-264; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:79-91).
- `GAP:input-autogrow-lock`: a multiline field that auto-grows 1→8 rows and then scrolls, with min/max rows that can be changed at runtime (locked during dictation; memo editor 8–28) (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:587-588; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:122-148).
- Caret geometry: the suggestion popup anchors at caret x − 12 pt. This is a GAP only if neither Input nor Editor exposes the caret rect (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:771).
- `GAP:typed-pasteboard-read`: UTI-aware NSPasteboard reads (file URLs first, refuse GIF before AppKit's synthesized TIFF, PNG > JPEG > TIFF with byte caps). Keep native_paste.rs in the Rust module instead of `nsh/clipboard` (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/native_paste.rs:62-172).
- `GAP:drop-hover-feedback`: drag-over tint on the composer pane. `onFileDrop` covers only the drop (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:3043-3050).
- `GAP:middle-ellipsis-measure`: file-card names keep the extension and cut the middle by measured width (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:1264-1305).
- `GAP:measure-based-overflow`: the toolbar folds controls into ⋯ by measured child widths and keeps row 1 unwrapped (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:738-895).
- Likely GAPs: a drawn arc for the context ring (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/toolbar.rs:283-320); a repeating opacity pulse and SF Symbol images on buttons (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:3087-3141); an accessibility Status/live region for the dictation overlay (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/panels.rs:2884-2902).

**Risks**
- Process-wide singletons have to live in the native module and be shared by every NSH window and JS context: draft ownership and the deleted-chat set (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:134-142), the single dictation owner (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/dictate.rs:71-75), the chat-tab ownership registry (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_tabs.rs:21-28), and the suggestion file and model caches (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/suggest_ui.rs:357).
- The draft store is a gpui `Global` inside riwork-desktop (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:704). It must move into a GPUI-free crate, keep its on-disk format byte for byte (`RIWORK_DRAFT_V1\n` + JSON, or plain text, in `chat-drafts/<uuid>`), and keep the quit drain and the reload flush (5 s).
- The send durability barrier (the recovery snapshot is fsynced before the provider sees the command) and the "clear only the same editor and generation" rule must stay inside one native call plus receipt event. Splitting them across JS awaits reopens the races covered by crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/draft_tests.rs:986 and :1017.
- Existing behavior to decide on before porting: `ChatView::open_deferred` (restored/background tabs) never calls `restore_draft`, so the first keystroke there overwrites the saved draft (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view.rs:497; crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:6426).
- Hidden chat tabs keep their live composer today, so a tab switch neither saves nor restores, and undo and IME state survive (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_drafts.rs:3). If NSH Tabs unmount inactive content, this changes.
- Offsets: the engine and Kit use UTF-8 byte offsets for caret, triggers, dictation and insertion. NSH fields likely use UTF-16. Convert at the bridge and test with emoji and CJK text.
- Do not port from the pure helpers where the live path differs. Live ⇧⏎ never approves for the session, even though `composer::enter` maps it (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/composer.rs:29-31 vs crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/editors.rs:218). There is no ↑-history recall: `composer::vertical/line_start/line_end` are unused (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/composer.rs:147-183).
- Security boundaries stay native: attachment staging (private dirs, O_NOFOLLOW, canonical UUIDs), sent-thumbnail reads and the image scope. TSX gets only scoped URLs or bytes and never resolves paths (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/image_scope.rs:1-23; crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/sent.rs:56-70).
- Quick Look today is a `qlmanage -p` child process with copy cleanup and a 1 h sweep. Moving to an in-process QLPreviewPanel changes the lifecycle, so keep the "one panel; the same file is reused" rule (crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui/quick_look.rs:203-275).
- Detached work has to outlive its UI: handoff runs after its dialog hides (crates/riwork-desktop/src/handoff_dialog.rs:28-41), and the memo editor's save-on-drop is synchronous today (crates/riwork-desktop/src/memo_editor.rs:330). Both need native-owned tasks with generation guards and a quit flush.
- Keep the subtle rule tables in Rust (billing switch: 6 hint states and the mismatch/Restart/EnterKey clicks; contributor toggle; model_choice effort carry-over; the suggestion trigger and ranking shared with iOS through `ios/Tests/Fixtures/composer-suggestions.json`). That way desktop, phone and tests keep a single source.
- Test debt to recreate in NSH: no test for ⌘↩ send, drag and drop, the attachment picker, Quick Look, the dictation failure banner buttons, or the toolbar fold. Several tests named in GPUI_KIT_INPUT_CONTRACT.md:206-217 and :313 never existed. The receipt doc says 56 px draft thumbnails, while the code draws 64 pt cards (GPUI_KIT_DRAFT_IMAGE_PREVIEW_RECEIPT.md:30-33 vs crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:792).

**Not covered or only shallow:** usage chip rendering (chat_view/usage_chip.rs), the notice stack and the "Notices (N)" history, the reference-card editing UI (chat_view/references.rs), the transcript display modes beyond the toolbar toggle, Settings panes beyond the dictation mic and delegation rows, and the engine internals of handoff, delegation and balancing (cited only).

## Panels, settings, dashboard & controls (P-PANEL)

Scope: `crates/riwork-desktop/src/` panels.rs, settings.rs, project_settings.rs, project_creator.rs, account_status.rs (+tests), api_key.rs, dashboard.rs (+decision_review.rs, tests.rs), schedule_panel.rs, controls.rs, behavior_controls.rs (+tests), tooltip.rs, icons.rs. Usage panel and panel-action handlers live in main.rs/global_window.rs and are cited where they define the behavior. All line numbers were read back from source at HEAD `50b3b41`.

Two cross-cutting facts that shape every row:
- **Two visual themes.** Each control has a Native (macOS-like: SF Symbols, capsules, sentence case) and a "colorful" theme branch (Menlo, outlined boxes, `02 / 05 PROJECTS`, `● LIVE`, upper case), switched by `ui_text::is_native()` through `controls::native` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/controls.rs:34`). Rows below give Native strings first and colorful strings in Notes. Whether NSH keeps the colorful themes is a product decision (see Gaps).
- **Polling, not events.** Project windows tick every 2 s (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:2298`). The global window also ticks every 2 s (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:648`). The Automations panel ticks every 1 s (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/schedule_panel.rs:293`). Every tick re-reads the store and settings files. Bridges should keep these cadences, or replace them with change events that are at least as fresh.
