### Bridge needs (P-PANEL)

**Store / projects**
- `Store.snapshot() -> State` [sync] and `Store.subscribe` [event]. Wraps `crates/riwork-core/src/store.rs:692`. Today: a 2 s poll per window (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:2298` → `refresh` :5623), plus an immediate `refresh_project_metadata` after each local mutation (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:3236`).
- `Store.projectTree(query, collapsed, order) -> rows` [sync]. Wraps `project_tree`/`project_search` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/panels.rs:328`, :483) and `sorted_project_indices` (`crates/riwork-engine/src/project_sort.rs:11`). The tree code is desktop-only today; move it to core.
- `Store.canMove(item, dest) -> bool` [sync]. Wraps `ProjectDropContext::accepts` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/panels.rs:537`).
- Folder, project and memo mutations, all [async] and each returning the updated record or a user-facing error string:
  - `moveProject` → `crates/riwork-core/src/store.rs:1316`
  - `moveFolder` → :1254
  - `createFolder` → :1207
  - `renameFolder` → :1234
  - `removeFolder` → :1282
  - `updateProjectMetadata` → :1360
  - `setProjectNotifications` → :1392
  - `setProjectOrchestratorWakes` → :1412
  - `setProjectCodexAccount` → :1485
  - `setProjectOrchestrator`, `setProjectDelegationPolicy`, `setProjectMemo`, `useProject` (:1195)
- `Projects.resolveFolder(text)` [sync], `Projects.inspect(path) -> Inspection` [async, caller debounces 250 ms], `Projects.create(path, name?, initGit) -> Project` [async]. Wrap `crates/riwork-desktop/src/project_creator.rs:36`, :92 and `crates/riwork-core/src/store.rs:974`. `Paths.ensureDefaultProjectsDirectory` / `defaultNewProjectPath` wrap `crates/riwork-core/src/paths.rs:64`, :71.
- `Recency.subscribe -> {projectId: lastEditUnix}` [event]. Wraps `crates/riwork-core/src/project_recency.rs:133`. Asked ≤ every 30 s per window (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:2396`), driven by FSEvents.
- `Worktrees.presence(paths)` [event]. Wraps the `WorktreePresence` cache (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/panels.rs:121-190`), 2 s freshness; the `stat` must run off the UI thread.

**Sessions & usage**
- `Sessions.subscribe -> {shells, cwds, metrics, activity}` [event]. Today the 2 s window tick (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:5787` `refresh_sessions`).
- `Sessions.chatCatalog(projectId, generation, force)` [async/event]. Wraps `crates/riwork-engine/src/session_catalog.rs:123`, :144, :334; 2 s interval, 2 s deadline, single-flight.
- `Usage.subscribe`, `Usage.refresh(force)`, `Usage.segments(mode, focus)`, `Usage.setVisibleTargets(ids)`. Wrap `crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:11806`, :13179, `crates/riwork-engine/src/usage.rs:116`, `crates/riwork-desktop/src/account_status.rs:248`. Today: the 2 s tick gated by per-home backoff (15 min / 60 s); Grok every 30 s while visible.

**Settings, accounts, keys, Cua, remote**
- `Settings.get()` [sync], `Settings.update(op)` [async, named ops, never a whole-object write], `Settings.subscribe` [event]. Wrap `crates/riwork-core/src/settings.rs:693`, :727, :738. Today: a 2 s poll with a file-stamp cache (`crates/riwork-core/src/settings.rs:697`). Named ops needed: `set(key, value)`, `stepTextSize` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/ui_text.rs:231`), `flipTerminalColors`, `chooseOrchestrator*`, `delegation.*`, `setStatusBar`, `setProjectOrder`.
- `Accounts.list` / `refresh` / `subscribe` / `selectDefault` / `defaultChoices(projectId)` / `resolve`. Wrap `crates/riwork-core/src/codex_accounts.rs:114` and `crates/riwork-desktop/src/account_status.rs:37`, :73, :449. Refresh triggers: startup (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13722`), Settings shown, the button, menu open. Never periodic.
- `ApiKey.status` / `save` / `remove` / `subscribe`. Wrap `crates/riwork-engine/src/anthropic_key.rs:64` and `crates/riwork-desktop/src/api_key.rs:41`, :58, :66; re-asked when Settings is shown (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/settings.rs:969`).
- `Cua.status` / `run(action)` / `subscribe`. Wrap `crates/riwork-engine/src/cua.rs:446`, :720. Today: a 15 s poll while not ready (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:13733`).
- `Remote.hosts` / `subscribe` / `removeHost` / `folders` / `projectLists`. Remote reader to own; consumed by PANEL-061/062/177/178.
- `OrcaImport.preview` / `import`. Wrap `crates/riwork-core/src/orca_import.rs:86`, :129.

**Dashboard & decisions**
- `Dashboard.subscribe -> {overview, observations, backends, automations, sampledAt, sampleError}` [event] and `Dashboard.refresh(force)` [async]. Wrap `crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:822` `sample` and :943 `refresh_dashboard` (`global_overview::observed::read`, `read_backends`, `wire_with_observations`). Today: a 2 s sample, a 10 s extras read while visible (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/main.rs:1349`), a 10 s redraw heartbeat.
- `Dashboard.intelligence` [event, separate]. Wraps `riwork_engine::dashboard_intelligence::enrich` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:988`); up to 8 s, rate-limited 1/min and 120/day.
- `Dashboard.layout(width)`, `visibleProjects(query, filter, sort)`, `taskLabels(tasks, width)` [sync]. Pure functions in `crates/riwork-desktop/src/dashboard.rs:231`, :296, :753: port them to TSX or expose them.
- `Decisions.send(decisionId, command) -> Ok | {message, uncertain}` [async]. Wraps `send_checked` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/dashboard/decision_review.rs:228`); move it to the engine.
- `GlobalWindow.saveState({filter, sort})` [async]. Wraps `save_state` (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/global_window.rs:1394`).

**Schedules**
- `Schedules.list` / `targets(scope)` / `save(prev?, draft)` / `pause(id, rev, paused)` / `delete(id, rev)` / `subscribe`. Wrap `crates/riwork-engine/src/schedules.rs:597`, :610, :680, :714 and `Target::bind_new_chat` / `bind`. Today: a 1 s poll only while the tab is on screen (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/schedule_panel.rs:293`, :341).

### Host needs (P-PANEL)

- `host:pickFolder({prompt: "Choose project folder", directories: true, multiple: false})`: NSOpenPanel for the creator (PANEL-047).
- `host:openProjectWindow(projectId | route)` and `host:openGlobalWindow(view)`: ↗ on project rows, dashboard routing, global window (PANEL-033/085/099).
- `host:panelVisibility(tabId) -> onScreen` [event]: gates Usage/Grok polling, the Automations 1 s loop, the dashboard 10 s reads and Project settings refresh (PANEL-066/090/114/075).
- `host:hintPanel`: a non-activating NSPanel tooltip window above the libghostty terminal NSViews. Needed only if the nsh/base Tooltip overlay cannot draw above embedded AppKit views (PANEL-123). The same question applies to the sort menu, account menu and dialogs drawn over terminals.
- `host:secureTextField`, if NSH Input has no secure mode that blocks copy and cut (PANEL-166).
- Keychain access stays in Rust (security_framework, `crates/riwork-engine/src/anthropic_key.rs`). A host channel is needed only if the NSH sandbox blocks it.

### Gaps & risks (P-PANEL)

- **In-app drag and drop (high).** Projects and folders are reorganized by dragging, with a custom preview chip, validated drop targets and hover feedback (PANEL-036/037). NSH 0.3 exposes only `onFileDrop` for external files: `GAP:in-app drag source/drop target`. Fallback: a "Move to…" Menu on rows and folders.
- **Missing nsh-base primitives.** No pressed `Toggle` (bell/wake toggles, Fast mode, Pause, colorful sort direction), no `ToggleGroup`/segmented control (dashboard filters, sorts and regions, Sessions filters, settings choice rows), no stepper. Compose them from Button/RadioGroup, but the AX "toggled" state and roving tab-stop semantics must be re-verified.
- **Tooltip deltas.** nsh/base Tooltip matches the 500 ms delay, but lacks the 8 s auto-hide (needs controlled `open` + timer), defaults to top placement with a 4 pt offset (RiWork prefers below with 8 pt margins and never under the pointer), and its nested-anchor rule is unknown. Whether its overlay draws above the AppKit terminal views is the biggest visual risk (PANEL-121–123).
- **Logic trapped in the desktop crate.** Project tree and search, drop validation, worktree presence, dashboard layout/filter/sort/`task_labels`, attention copy, decision `validate`/`send_checked`, and account resolution (`account_status.rs`) are pure Rust in `riwork-desktop`. Move them to core or engine before the bridge, or they will be re-implemented in TSX and drift.
- **Polling everywhere.** Polling is widespread: 2 s window tick (store, settings, sessions, appearance), 1 s Automations, 10 s dashboard extras, 30 s recency, 15 s Cua, per-home usage backoff, 2 s presence probes. Bridges should push events but keep the visibility gating and single-flight/generation guards that tests pin (PANEL-058, PANEL-114).
- **Window-wide search state.** One query string is synced into every panel's field (PANEL-006). Per-component TSX state would silently break this.
- **Custom keyboard engines.** Project settings, Automations and Settings each run their own Tab order and arrow-key logic (PANEL-074/113/137/151/156). There is no arrow-key roving in sidebar lists; Tab only. NSH native focus order will differ unless pinned. Settings `focus_order` already omits two groups (PANEL-138).
- **Nested focusable controls inside a focusable row button** (project rows, folder headings, dashboard rows): verify that NSH AppKit AX allows this without double activation (PANEL-029).
- **Two visual themes.** Every control has Native and colorful branches (different strings: "02 / 05 PROJECTS", "● LIVE", uppercase). Decide whether NSH ships colorful themes; the parity rows assume Native strings.
- **Global UI scale.** Every size is multiplied by the user text-size setting (9–24 pt). `GAP:global UI type scale` unless NSH theming supports it.
- **Destructive actions without confirmation.** Remove folder is immediate (PANEL-035). Automation delete and remote host remove use two-click confirms (PANEL-106/178). Keep these exactly.
- **Doc drift.** docs/automations.md:5 says "Codex or Claude", but the Automations editor also offers Muse (`crates/riwork-desktop/src/schedule_panel.rs:1127-1130`).
- **Proposed tests that do not exist in source:**
  - `native_project_actions_reveal_for_keyboard_scope_and_keep_pointer_hover` and `sort_popover_keyboard_reselect_dismissal_and_refresh_preserve_focus` (GPUI_KIT_CONTROLS_PROPOSED_TESTS.json:9-10)
  - `projects_search_has_readable_bounds_and_placeholder_in_narrow_{colorful,native}_panels` and `projects_search_pointer_editing_…_colorful` (GPUI_KIT_PROJECT_SEARCH_PROPOSED_TESTS.json:6-8)

  Their behaviors (sort-menu keyboard and dismissal, action-strip reveal, narrow search geometry) currently have no automated coverage.
- **Other untested behaviors.** No automated tests for: the project creator UI flow (debounce, Cmd+G, Cmd+O, path follows name), the folder editor dialog, task detail, remote panel rows, the Usage panel, "SUBSCRIPTIONS (AUTO)", the remote two-click remove, the Orca import UI, the Cua label/action table, and the usage backoff intervals.
- **Perf.** GPUI lays out every row each frame; the panels rely on `pane_layout::laid_out_alone` and redraw-on-change guards (Project settings, Automations, dashboard heartbeat). NSH List virtualization should be used for Projects, Sessions and the dashboard table, with stable keys.
- **Not covered here.** Nuke-shells confirmation (`nuke_shells.rs`); remote transport and pairing internals; status_bar.rs item rendering beyond the settings rows; memo editor internals; `chat_display_modes` (persisted but not shown in Settings).

# P-DOCS — normative requirements from contracts, receipts and docs (GPUI → NSH parity)

Repo: `/Users/dominik/orca/projects/riWork-nsh-contracts` (branch `nsh-contracts`, HEAD `50b3b41`). Read-only extraction. Every doc citation is `FILE:line` (verified by reading). Code/test citations are `path:line` relative to the repo root (`crates/...`, `remote/...`); "not found" = the named contract item is not present in the current tree (renamed/removed/never landed).

Scope notes
- GPUI_KIT_* files are worker receipts from 2026-10-06/07. Many of their requirements exist only to work around gpui-kit/gpui-base 0.7.1 / gpui-pre 0.3.8 behavior; those rows are marked `n/a-NSH`, and the Notes column states the **behavior that NSH must still hold**.
- Roadmap items marked "Planned" in `docs/chat-improvement-roadmap.md` (items 10–25) and the unimplemented parts of `inline-timeline-design-20261014.md` are skipped. Implemented parts of the inline-timeline design (worker markers, `NoteWorker`) are included via `docs/chat-events.md`.
- `docs/remote-protocol*.md` is mostly implemented by the `remote/` connector + `riwork` CLI (UI-independent; survives the rewrite unchanged). Only rows that touch the desktop app, or that the CLI must keep for the connector, are listed; the full wire protocol is covered by `remote/tests` and fixtures.
- NSH surface vocabulary as briefed, plus `nsh/base:Editor|TextView|DiffView|DataTable|Table|Toast|Plot|Collapsible` (nsh-base 0.3 port of gpui-base).

Legend for Verify: `core` = pure Rust test in core/engine/cli/remote survives unchanged · `bridge` = test at the native-module boundary · `nsh-test` = NSH component/screen test · `host` = AppKit host-channel test · `manual` = Cua.ai Driver desktop acceptance.

### Gaps & risks (P-DOCS)

1. **Terminal key/clipboard routing** (DOCS-005/006/014/127): today Cmd+X/C/V/A, Tab, Cmd+=/−/0 and Cmd+V-with-files are arbitrated between GPUI keymaps, the macOS menu key-equivalent path and Ghostty's raw key callback. In NSH the AppKit `performKeyEquivalent:` chain, NSH keymap contexts and the libghostty NSView must reproduce "terminal gets the key first, app menu only as fallback, file/picture paste intercepted first". Highest-risk item; needs a host-level test.
2. **Transcript selection over a virtualized list** (DOCS-064–067): the GPUI solution needed one document-level selection participant outside virtual rows plus a pure exporter. NSH `SelectionArea` must support cross-renderer (TextView, DiffView, code, tables) reading order, survive unmounting, and copy without trimming. If nsh-base inherits gpui-base's per-element registration sweep, the PRE-03 bug returns. Keep `selection_document.rs` as a pure native module.
3. **Editor parity** (DOCS-018–025, 088, 091): IME-marked-text guards for Return/Escape/Tab, UTF-8 vs UTF-16 offsets, undoable programmatic range replace (dictation), caret rect for `/`/`@`/`&` popovers, secure masked input (pairing link, API key). Confirm nsh/base:Editor exposes each; otherwise GAP.
4. **Perf budgets are not currently green**: the 25 ms large-turn frame guard fails on baseline and current GPUI (33.6–40.5 ms, Debug, loaded host). Pick an NSH target explicitly (release vs Debug) rather than silently inheriting a failing guard; keep the structural guards (< 100 materialized rows, no token reprojection, < 1.5 ms/event) which are machine-independent.
5. **Polling discipline**: NSH must keep every tmux/ps/lsof/store read off the UI thread and keep visibility gating (hidden windows, covered Schedules tab, covered recency scan). A JS/TSX polling loop per screen would regress idle CPU (baseline 0.65 % core idle).
6. **Desktop-only writers**: `appearance.json`, `project-recency.json`, worker-marker finals (`NoteWorker` for terminals + 30 s late notes), preview GUI-owner mailbox side, terminal tab auto-adoption (2 s), chat drafts — the phone/CLI depend on the desktop app producing these. Each needs an explicit owner in the NSH app.
7. **AppKit host features with no NSH 0.3 API known**: Dock menu (DOCS-117), UNUserNotification posting/claiming only from packaged app (DOCS-152), Quick Look (DOCS-175), NSOpenPanel/NSSavePanel (DOCS-032/093), Keychain (DOCS-161), custom titlebar drag regions + 78×28 traffic-light island (DOCS-111), PDF page rendering (DOCS-122), frame-duration logging equivalent to `ZED_MEASUREMENTS` (DOCS-221), Mermaid helper process (DOCS-096). Treat as `host:` channels or GAPs.
8. **Doc/code drift found**: (a) draft thumbnail size 56 px in receipt vs `CARD_SIDE = 64.` in attachment_ui.rs:792; (b) many GPUI_KIT proposed test names do not exist (see list below) — the contracts overstate test coverage; (c) docs/remote-protocol.md changelog 2026-10-01 says waiting `shell.output` uses a tmux control-mode client, but sessions.rs:5532 returns `None` (polling mitigation) — protocol doc is stale; (d) shared-tabs-review-report.md:18 documents Pin/Unpin, superseded by shared-tabs.md:3 "no pins"; (e) README.md:507 still lists ⇧⏎ = allow for session while GPUI_KIT_CHAT_PLAN.md:117-118 says that hint was removed because Shift/Alt+Enter is newline — verify current behavior before porting.
9. **AX/automation IDs**: QA relies on Cua and stable IDs (DOCS-059); GPUI bodies lacked AX so tests used screenshot pixels. NSH native AX is an upgrade, but the stable identifiers must be carried over or Cua scripts break.

Contract test names referenced in GPUI_KIT_* receipts but **not found** in `crates/` (grep `fn <name>`): `root_tab_traversal_uses_base_focus_and_skips_disabled_controls`, `root_modal_tab_controls_stay_within_base_focus_trap`, `base_toggle_switch_link_share_pointer_and_keyboard_owner_callbacks`, `accessible_nodes_keep_names_states_and_only_enabled_click_actions`, `root_preserves_content_identity_and_selection_is_window_local`, `root_modal_scope_and_input_terminal_keys_do_not_cross_owner_boundaries`, `content_control_typography_and_row_bounds_match_legacy_at_scale_and_palette`, `composing_escape_defers_base_clean_on_escape_until_plain_escape`, `marked_tab_and_shift_tab_keep_child_focus_until_owner_navigation_is_safe`, `focused_child_and_parent_ignore_an_unfocused_siblings_marked_range`, `glyph_hit_testing_drag_selection_and_compact_wrapped_layout`, `kit_duplicate_filters_keep_distinct_persistent_geometry`, `kit_pair_fields_retain_supplied_defaults`, `kit_removed_filter_rejects_stale_events_and_reopens_without_subscriptions`, `kit_visible_filters_protect_composition_history_and_focus`, `base_word_line_and_shift_extension_use_real_pointer_events`, `rich_code_table_and_tool_output_participate_in_one_library_selection`, `focused_composer_model_and_request_answer_keep_copy_priority`, `wrapped_unicode_drag_survives_a_wheel_repaint`, `selecting_a_path_does_not_open_it_but_a_collapsed_click_does`, `same_length_diff_replacement_changes_visible_copy_and_clears_old_selection`, `source_projection_keeps_code_blank_lines_trailing_and_whitespace_only_bytes`, `base_pointer_transfers_editor_focus_before_transcript_copy_without_losing_draft`, These behaviors need new NSH tests regardless.

Not covered by this reader: full v1/v2 crypto/wire details of docs/remote-protocol.md (lines 315–2297: direct typing, live terminal, deep scrollback, link, chat/orchestrator/upload/dashboard/memo extensions) beyond desktop-touching rows — they are connector/CLI-owned and verified by `remote/tests` + fixtures; docs/remote-deployment.md (relay ops) and docs/remote-protocol-v2-verification.md (historical counts) summarized only; iOS-specific numbers in docs/tool-ui-performance.md:145-216 excluded from baselines.

## Bridge needs → frozen modules

Readers proposed bridge functions in working names (each section's “Bridge needs”). They map to
the frozen modules of [contracts §1](nsh-contracts.md#1-riworknative-module-surface) as follows;
function lists are filled per module by the port task that first uses them (contracts §1.10).

| Readers' working names | Frozen module / surface |
|---|---|
| `Store.*`, `Projects.*`, project tree/search/sort | `Store` (§1.5) |
| `Sessions.*`, `Shells.create`, `Sessions.createHarness`, `Sessions.exit`, `Sessions.closeChoice`, worker sampling | `Sessions` (§1.7) |
| `ProjectTabs.*`, shared tabs `moveFromDrop`/`moves` | `Tabs` (§1.7) |
| `Chat.subscribe`, `Chat.send`, `Chat.toggle`/`expandAll`/`setMode`/`reveal`/`anchor`, `Chat.copyText`, `Chat.selectionLeaves`/`exportSelection`, `Find.search`, `Chat.turnIndex`, activity clock | `Chat` + `ChatHandle` (§1.6) |
| `ChatTabs.claim/release/reserve/finish/epoch` | `Chat.claimTab` / `Chat.releaseTab` (§1.10) |
| `Shells.claim/release` | native `riwork.terminal` claim registry (§3.5) |
| `Schedules.*`, `Background.start` (schedule dispatcher) | `Schedules` (§1.8) + host `ScheduleTicker` (§2.5) |
| `Layouts.load/save/saveTabLayout/saveChatState`, `Layouts.preserveLocked/gather/applyDefault/planPreview/planBesideReveal/planMainReveal/reuseOpenTab/carryLockedRegions/windowFrame`, `Workspace.loadProject`, binary ↔ n-ary conversion | `Layouts` / `LayoutSession` (§4) |
| `Settings.get/update/subscribe`, `Settings.changeTextSize`, `Settings.setChatDisplay` | `Settings` (§1.10) |
| `Appearance.get/subscribe/setSystemDark` | `Appearance` (§1.10) |
| `Usage.codex/grok` | `Usage` |
| `Activity.subscribe` | `Activity` |
| `GlobalWindow.loadState/saveState`, `Dashboard.readExtras/enrich/route` | `Dashboard` |
| `Orchestrators.*` | `Orchestrators` |
| `Workers.subscribe/cardStatus/shelf/action/summarize` | `Workers` |
| `ChatDrafts.*`, `Drafts.addReference/referenceStates/reanchor` | `Drafts` (flush on quit/reload is host-internal, §2.5) |
| `Markdown.*`, `Diff.*`, `Diagram.*`, `CommandLog.*`, `Media.*` | Same-named modules (§1.10); chat rows already carry parsed blocks (§1.6) |
| `Files.localTarget/resolveChatTarget/canOpenExcerpt/validateAction/exportBytes`, `Links.cellAt`, file explorer and preview reads | `Files`; terminal link resolution stays inside `riwork.terminal` (§3.3) |
| `Plan.*`, `Review.*`, `Feedback.*` | `Chat` (plan documents, turn review and feedback operate on a chat's items) |
| `Remote.select/tick/createShell/createProject/orchestrators` | `Remote` |
| `Preview.*` (app preview, preview control) | `Preview` |
| `ProjectRecency.scan` | `ProjectRecency` |
| `Runtime.*`, `Cli.run`, `Workspace.resolveStartup`, `Dock.items`, `Background.start` | Host-internal, no JS surface (§2.5) |
| `Perf.span` | Debug-only diagnostics; not part of the frozen surface |

## Host needs → frozen host names

Readers wrote host needs in working names. This table maps each to the frozen surface in
[`docs/nsh-contracts.md`](nsh-contracts.md). “In-window” means the window's own TSX plus its
`riwork/native` `LayoutSession` handle it; no AppKit host call is involved.

| Reader's working name (section) | Frozen surface |
|---|---|
| `host.params` `windowKind`, `projectId`, `startupPath`, `restore`, `startupNotice`, `forcedAppearance` (SHELL) | `host.params` `kind`, `projectId`, `path`, `restore`, `notice`, `appearance` (§2.2) |
| `openProjectWindow`, `openGlobalWindow` + `showView` (SHELL, PANEL) | `window.open` (`kind`, `view`, `start`) + event `global.show` (§2.3, §2.4) |
| `windows.list` (SHELL) | `window.list` |
| `routeToProject` + `step`, `openProjectMemo`, `openSettings({section:"apiKey"})` across windows (SHELL, CHATC, CHATT) | `window.route` + event `route` (`step`: `showShell`, `chat`, `task`, `panel`, `apiKeySettings`, `projectMemo`) |
| `collectRuntimeWindows` (SHELL) | Host-internal: the host reads each window's `LayoutSession` in Rust (§2.5, §4.5) |
| `quit`, `willQuit`, `windowWillClose`/`windowClosed`, `appWillTerminate`, `reload` (SHELL, CHATC) | `app.quit`, `app.restart`, event `window.willClose`; drafts and layouts are flushed by the host in Rust (§2.5) |
| `windowActivated`/`Deactivated`, `occlusionChanged`, `appActive`, `windowFrameChanged`, `backingScaleChanged`, `windowState`, `panelVisibility` (SHELL, TERM, PANEL) | event `window.state` + DockArea `onPanelActiveChange` (in-window) |
| `windowPlacement`, `displayVisibleFrame` (SHELL) | `window.placement` |
| Window chrome: transparent title bar, traffic lights (10, 8), min 640 × 400, drag regions, title-bar double-click (SHELL) | Host-internal at window creation; TSX drag regions via `desktop={{ windowDrag: true }}` (§2.5) |
| Application menu and its commands (SHELL) | Host-owned menu → event `command` (§2.4) |
| `dock.setMenu`, `dock.updateWindow`, `dock.activateWindow`, `dock.newWindow` (SHELL) | Host-internal Dock menu; `window.setDockInfo` post (§2.3) |
| `postNotification` + `notificationResponse` (SHELL) | `system.notify` (with `actions`) + event `notification.activate` |
| `setAppAppearance`, `listFonts` (SHELL) | `app.setAppearance`, `system.fonts` |
| `appearance` change (TERM, CHATT) | `Settings.subscribe` (RiWork theme) + `nsh/device` `colorScheme` + event `appearance` |
| `pickFolder`, `openPanel` (PANEL, CHATC) | `system.openPanel` |
| `savePanel` (CHATT) | `system.savePanel` |
| `quickLook.show/close`, Quick Look for sent attachments (CHATC, CHATT) | `system.quickLook` (`paths: []` closes) |
| `revealInFinder`, `openWithSystem` (TERM, CHATT) | `system.revealInFinder`, `system.openWith` |
| `sharePicker` (TERM) | `system.share` |
| Typed NSPasteboard read (CHATC, TERM) | `system.readPasteboard` |
| Microphone / Speech permission prompts (CHATC) | `system.requestPermission`; the `riwork-speech` helper and usage strings are bundle configuration |
| “Open System Settings” URLs (CHATC) | `nsh/device` `openUrl`, allowed by the host's open-URL handler |
| `openPreview`, `openFile`, `openFileReadOnly`, `listShellTabs`, `focusShellTab`, `openWorkerShell`/`Chat`, `showUsagePanel`, `handOff`, `newChat`, `tabSummaryChanged` (CHATT, CHATC) | In-window (TSX + `LayoutSession`); `window.route` when the target is another window |
| Edit ▸ Copy to the transcript (CHATT) | Edit menu responder selectors forwarded as `command { id: "copy" }` (§2.4) |
| `keyEvents` (repeat, key-up), `inputComposition` (marked text), drag-enter/leave on drop targets, secure text field (CHATC, PANEL, TERM) | Not host calls: `GAP` in NSH `Input`/keymap/`onFileDrop` (see gap register); port gate |
| `hintPanel` tooltip window above terminals (PANEL) | Not needed if overlays draw above `riwork.terminal`; otherwise `riwork.terminal` `frozen` (§3.2) |
| `riwork.terminal` requirements: command/cwd/quiet login, theme override, focus on spawn, `cmd:respawn`/`reattach`, snapshot/frozen, `attachFailed`/`exited` (SHELL, TERM) | `riwork.terminal` `target` (command and cwd derived natively), `colors`, `focusOnAttach`, `cmd:reattach`, `frozen`, `event:state` (§3) |
| `riwork.preview` app-preview frame view (TERM) | Reserved native component (§3.6) |
| Keychain (PANEL) | Stays in Rust behind `riwork/native` (`Accounts`) |
| Window title for remote projects (TERM) | `nsh/desktop` `Window.setTitle` |
| Pinch/magnify, ⌃-scroll in file preview (TERM) | `GAP` if `nsh/gesture` lacks magnify (see gap register) |

## Doc/code disagreements to settle before porting

Port the behavior of the code unless the owner of the feature decides otherwise; then fix the
doc or the code first, so the NSH port has one target. Items marked *bug* are code behavior that
looks unintended and should be fixed (or explicitly kept) before the port copies it.

| # | Where | Disagreement | Rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `GPUI_KIT_DRAFT_IMAGE_PREVIEW_RECEIPT.md:10,31` vs `crates/riwork-desktop/src/chat_view/attachment_ui.rs:792` | Receipt promises a 56 px draft thumbnail; code draws `CARD_SIDE = 64.` | CHATC attachments, DOCS composer |
| 2 | `GPUI_KIT_*` receipts and `*_PROPOSED_TESTS.json` | About 40 test names cited as contract tests do not exist under `crates/` (lists in the DOCS and PANEL gap sections). Those behaviors are untested today; give them `nsh-test` or `bridge` tests during the port. | DOCS, PANEL |
| 3 | `docs/remote-protocol.md` | Describes tmux control-mode reads; control-mode watching is disabled and every caller polls (`crates/riwork-engine/src/sessions/watch.rs:1-4`). | TERM remote, DOCS remote |
| 4 | `docs/shared-tabs-review-report.md:11,18` vs `docs/shared-tabs.md:3` | The review report still lists Pin/Unpin; the current contract has no pins. | SHELL shared tabs, DOCS shared tabs |
| 5 | `README.md:507` vs composer code | README: with an empty box ⇧⏎ allows for the session. Code: ⇧⏎ never approves for the session (the pure helper allows it, the live key path does not). | CHATC send/approval |
| 6 | `docs/automations.md:5,7` vs `crates/riwork-desktop/src/schedule_panel.rs:1129` | Doc says “Codex or Claude”; the editor also offers Muse. | PANEL schedules |
| 7 | `crates/riwork-desktop/src/tab_close_dialog.rs:70` | Dialog says “reopen it from ＋ › Open a worker or shell”; the strip has no ＋ any more. | SHELL tab close |
| 8 | composer drafts (*bug*) | Restored background tabs (`open_deferred`) never restore their draft, so the first keystroke overwrites the saved one. | CHATC drafts |
| 9 | composer history | Pure helpers implement ↑ history recall; the live composer has none. Port the live behavior. | CHATC editing |
| 10 | `PERFORMANCE_RECOVERY_REPORT.txt:115-121,312-318,418` | The 25 ms large-turn frame guard fails on the GPUI baseline itself (33.6-40.5 ms, Debug, loaded host). Pick an explicit NSH target instead of inheriting a failing guard. | DOCS perf, CHATT perf |

## Gap register

Gaps grouped by what would close them. “Contract” means `docs/nsh-contracts.md` already decides
how the port handles it; “Upstream” means NSH 0.3 needs an API it does not have (request it, or
build a RiWork native component); “App” means TSX can build it from existing primitives. The
machine index after this table lists every `GAP:` tag with its rows.

| Theme | Gap | Rows | Closed by | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Text input | IME composition (marked-text) state visible to key handlers, so ⏎/⎋/Tab/↑↓ are not stolen mid-composition | CHATC-007, CHATC-070, CHATC-096, CHATC-145, CHATC-174 | Upstream (`Input`/`Editor`) | Open, **port gate** |
| Text input | Key interception before the field (↑/↓/⏎/Tab/⎋ to the suggestion list, submit on ⏎ with ⇧/⌥ newline) with key-repeat and key-up | CHATC-005, CHATC-020, CHATC-079 | Upstream (`Input`/keymap) | Open, **port gate** |
| Text input | Cancellable paste (images/files become attachments, not text) | CHATC-013, CHATC-101 | Upstream (`Input onPaste`); typed read via `system.readPasteboard` (contract §2.3) | Open |
| Text input | Auto-grow 1-8 rows, lockable during dictation; programmatic edit as one undo step | CHATC-001, CHATC-140, CHATC-142 | Upstream (`Input`) or `nsh/base:Editor` | Open |
| Text input | Secure text field that blocks copy/cut | CHATC-049, PANEL-166 | Upstream (`Input` secure mode) | Open |
| Keyboard | Keymap context precedence (window shortcuts before the terminal except ⌘= ⌘- ⌘0 ⌘C ⌘A), Ctrl-Tab capture | SHELL-104, SHELL-243 | Contract §2.4 (menu/key-equivalent order) + `nsh/keymap` | Partly; Ctrl-Tab needs a check |
| Pointer | Middle-click on tabs | SHELL-125 | Upstream (`nsh/gesture` button) | Open |
| Drag and drop | In-app drag sources and drop targets with custom preview (projects, folders) | PANEL-036 | Upstream; fallback “Move to…” menu | Open |
| Drag and drop | Drag-enter/leave feedback for file drops | CHATC-105 | Upstream (`onFileDrop` hover) | Open |
| Layout | Locked groups keep pixel size on window resize | SHELL-084 | Contract §4.3 rule 5 + `setExtent`; upstream `fixed` per split child | Covered; exact live-resize needs upstream |
| Layout | Keep one empty group (main) | SHELL-090 | Contract §4.3 rule 3 (sentinel panel) | Covered |
| Layout | Top root dock and RiWork's 23 % / 12 pt drop zones | SHELL-107 | Upstream (DockArea root-edge targets, custom thresholds) | Open |
| Layout | Fixed leading tab segment, tab overflow menu, header inset under traffic lights | SHELL-026, SHELL-115, SHELL-129 | App (`renderTab`, `tabBarStyle`, `titleBar()`) | App work |
| Window chrome | Traffic-light position, window-drag regions inside content | SHELL-017, SHELL-025, SHELL-131 | Contract §2.5 (host sets chrome); `desktop={{ windowDrag: true }}` | Covered |
| Window state | Occlusion, zoomed vs normal frame | SHELL-020, SHELL-030 | Contract §2.3 `window.placement`, §2.4 `window.state` | Covered |
| Window state | Metal drawable count (GPU memory) | SHELL-311 | n/a for AppKit views; only matters for an NSH Canvas/Metal layer | Check |
| AppKit | Dock menu, user notifications, typed pasteboard read | DOCS-117, DOCS-152, CHATC-102 | Contract §2.3/§2.5 (host) | Covered |
| Text rendering | Selection endpoints that survive recycled List cells; Rust-built copy | CHATT-238 | Contract §1.6 (Rust selection + `exportSelection`); upstream selection hooks for the visuals | Partly |
| Text rendering | Range highlight and range-rect query in TextView (find) | CHATT-264, CHATT-265 | Upstream (`TextView`) | Open |
| Text rendering | Text measurement in TSX; per-run truncation with separator-aware or middle ellipsis; measured overflow | CHATC-113, CHATC-157, CHATT-208, CHATT-213 | Upstream (measure API) or Rust measurement behind the bridge | Open |
| Visuals | SF Symbols with weight and tint; pulse animation; context-meter ring | SHELL-308, CHATC-139, CHATC-155 | Upstream (`Image` symbol source); ring via `Canvas` (App) | Open |
| Controls | Pressed Toggle, ToggleGroup/segmented, stepper, AX “toggled” state | PANEL-031, PANEL-080, PANEL-119 | App (compose `Button`) + AX role upstream if missing | App work |
| Accessibility | Live status announcements for the dictation overlay | CHATC-138 | `nsh/accessibility` `announce` (check) | Check |
| Terminal | Overlays above `riwork.terminal` (else snapshot/freeze) | TERM-050, TERM-071, TERM-087 | Contract §3.2 `frozen` | **Port gate** |
| Terminal | ⌘-click and mouse-down interception before Ghostty | TERM-038, TERM-055 | Contract §3.1/§3.3 (link resolution inside the component) | Covered by design |
| Terminal | Re-parent without re-attach | TERM-014 | Contract §3.5 | **Port gate** |
| Terminal | Ghostty cell size for link geometry | TERM-052 | Native side (read Ghostty `CELL_SIZE` action, or keep RiWork's padding math) | Native work |
